# S8.6 — Operating suite and court clusters Implementation Plan

> **Status:** Done (controller, D-098; pending the owner's reading) · **Date:** 2026-10-02 · **Roadmap:** [S8.6](2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) (section S8, Typologies) · **Decisions:** D-009, D-026, D-061, D-062, D-063, D-064, D-065, D-079, D-085, D-087, D-088, D-090, D-091, D-092, D-097, D-098, D-099, D-102, D-103; [ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md) (the specification of the two families, Task 4), [ADR-008](../decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md) and [ADR-002](../decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md) (several holes, Task 4), [ADR-011](../decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md), [ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)
>
> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement S8.6 of D-098: the last two families of the four-source set with one plan for every storey, as ADR-006 specifies them: `SYN-TYP-015` (*Operating Suite*, theatre banks between alternating clean and dirty corridors, every bank and corridor adjoining the support rooms across the suite's west end, one zone per department, D-097) and `SYN-TYP-011` (*Court Cluster*, wings of stair bays around a row of at least two courts, each middle wing bordering two courts, the first footprint with several holes), with their Grasshopper components and icons. The theatre preset's WWR of 0 leaves the banks' east ends without a window and without `WindowOmitted` (ADR-011, rule 1), which the generator tests' window check now states. Every simplifier, aggregator, and validation already handles several holes; *Perimeter Core* gives each court its own court zones per orientation, which ADR-008 records. Both families validate with every simplifier and aggregator on their canonical plans, rotated and moved by two placements, with the fewest and the most tested counts, and with the most at the second placement. Every existing plan, floor, building, and snapshot stays unchanged; no `Lod.Core` file changes. S8.6 ends with version 0.8.5 and the tag `v0.8.5`; after the stage gate and a fresh clone of the tag pass `scripts/verify.ps1`, the controller pushes `main` and `v0.8.5` (D-098). The close-out also removes two stray code-fence lines that the S8.5 close-out left in the decision log, and checks the code fences of every Markdown file it touches.

**Architecture:** Each family is a folder `Lod.Generators/<Family>/` with a parameter record (a static `Default` holding the ADR-006 defaults), a preset record with one slot per space type, and a generator on the `PlanGenerator` base. `OperatingSuiteGenerator` stacks rectangular bands from south to north east of one support zone, so every shared edge is bit-identical. `CourtClusterGenerator` places its wings with `StairBayLayout` (`Wing`, `Fit`, `FitErrors`), as `EnclosedCourtGenerator` does, and stores one clockwise hole per court; no code is shared with `EnclosedCourtGenerator` beyond `StairBayLayout`, whose summary names the new user. The test helper `PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall` expects no window on a wall whose zone's preset has WWR 0. Two components derive from the S8.1 `TypologyGeneratorComponent`, and `scripts/make_icons.py` draws two new icons.

**Tech Stack:** C# 12 (`LangVersion` 12.0); .NET SDK ≥ 8 (`global.json` pins 8.0.100 with `rollForward: latestMajor`); `netstandard2.0` core libraries; Clipper2 2.0.0; RhinoCommon and Grasshopper 8.19.25132.1001 (plugin targets `net7.0`); xUnit 2.9.3, xunit.runner.visualstudio 3.1.5, Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk 17.14.1 (tests target `net8.0`); Windows PowerShell 5.1 for `scripts/verify.ps1`; Python 3.10 or later with Pillow for `scripts/make_icons.py` (the icons in this plan were drawn with Pillow 12.3.0).

## Global Constraints

- **Gate:** S8.5 is complete on `main` (`16ead1f`, tag `v0.8.4`, D-103), with D-102 and D-103 recorded; this plan is committed to `main` before Task 1 ("docs(plans): add the s8.6 operating suite and court cluster plan"); it is the only file of that commit. ADR-006 (with the S8.4 specification of the two families, their defaults, zone IDs, order, and validation), ADR-008, ADR-011, and ADR-014 are accepted. Before Task 1 the working tree is clean (`git status --short` prints nothing).
- Rhino 8 (8.19 or later) only (D-003); stages exchange Grasshopper objects, not JSON (D-017).
- `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` have no RhinoCommon or Grasshopper reference; their tests run with plain `dotnet test`.
- Nullable reference types on; warnings are errors; `.editorconfig` rules are enforced in the build; every public member of `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` has XML docs (CS1591 is an error).
- Generation is deterministic; outputs have a stable order: the operating suite lists `SUP`, then its bands from south to north, `DC1`, `TB1`, `CC1`, `TB2`, `DC2`, `TB3`, `CC2`, … (clean and dirty corridors numbered separately); the court cluster lists the south wing (`S-ST1…`, then `S-U1…`, west to east), the north wing, then the west wing, the middle wings `M1`, `M2`, …, and the east wing (south to north), each wing its stairs first, and stores its courts west to east.
- **Counts.** The operating suite needs at least 2 theatre banks and the court cluster at least 2 courts (one court is `SYN-TYP-005`); neither has an upper bound (ADR-006). A count below its minimum and every length that is not positive and finite are the error `InvalidParameter`, every error reported at once; the court cluster reports a stair bay that leaves no dwelling width with `StairBayLayout.FitErrors`, for its south and north wings only when the count is valid (their length depends on it).
- Numerical tolerances come only from `ToleranceSettings` (ADR-004); S8.6 adds none and changes none, and `ToleranceSettings.cs` is not touched. The minimum wall and window geometry stays in `GeometryLimits` (ADR-011): a wall whose zone's preset has WWR 0 has no glazed area, so it gets no window and no `WindowOmitted` (rule 1), unchanged.
- **Existing outputs do not change.** The twenty-two existing snapshots (`linear-plan-canonical`, `stack-two-storeys`, the three `simplifier-*`, the three `aggregator-*`, the S8.1 snapshots `stair-bay-bar-canonical`, `stair-pair-canonical`, `gallery-bar-canonical`, `terrace-row-canonical`, the S8.2 snapshots `point-plate-canonical`, `winged-band-canonical`, `open-court-canonical`, the S8.3 snapshot `enclosed-court-canonical`, the S8.4 snapshots `office-plate-canonical` and `cafeteria-canonical`, and the S8.5 snapshots `radial-lobes-canonical`, `branching-mall-canonical`, `care-hub-canonical`, and `foyer-halls-canonical`) stay byte-identical, and every existing test keeps passing; no test is removed, and no existing icon PNG changes. `EnclosedCourtGenerator` is not touched.
- **Never weaken a check (AGENTS.md rules 11, 12).** Tasks 1 and 2 start from tests that fail to build, the way each task says; the integration matrices pass at once, and each task says to stop if a case fails. The change to `PlanAssertions` of Task 1 is stricter, not weaker: a wall whose zone's preset has WWR 0 must have no window, where the helper before would have required one; for every other wall the check is unchanged, and Task 1 runs every earlier generator test against it before the new tests are added. No tolerance is widened, no validation check is relaxed, and no test is weakened or removed.
- **Report, never repair (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6).** A plan that *Perimeter Core* cannot zone fails with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087), never repaired; the generators do not impose that precondition, and Task 2 tests it on 9 m wings. Zones about 1 m wide on rotated plans remain the known limit of D-090; S8.6 does not change it.
- **Typed records per family** (AGENTS.md): each family has its own parameter and preset records; there is no catch-all parameter class and no dictionary of parameters. The presets are `OperatingTheatre`, `CleanCorridor`, `DirtyCorridor`, and `ClinicalSupport` (015), and `DwellingUnit` and `Stair` (011), each checked against its slot's space type (`PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`). No space type and no preset is added (ADR-014 already holds them).
- **Department zoning (D-097).** A non-residential zone is one department: each theatre bank, each corridor, and the support rooms together are one zone each. Housing keeps one zone per dwelling and storey (D-009, D-085).
- **Source contract (D-093).** ADR-006 and `docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md` hold everything this plan needs; `typology-synthesizer` is not opened while this plan is executed.
- **No backward compatibility (D-064).** Two components are added with new GUIDs: *Operating Suite* `33323afd-abf1-4ecc-bf54-5d67c411c962` and *Court Cluster* `01fc2ca9-3ec0-4142-b827-2519a4523d36`. No existing component, input, output, or GUID changes.
- Grasshopper components are thin adaptors (AGENTS.md): every rule lives in `Lod.Core` or `Lod.Generators`; a component reads inputs, calls the generator, and reports its diagnostics.
- Research equivalence rules are not changed (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 7): S8.6 adds generators and changes no step of the pipeline, so the research documentation needs no edit; ADR-008 and ADR-002 record that several holes need no change.
- **Code fences.** Every Markdown file this plan touches keeps balanced code fences: every opening fence line carries a language, every closing fence line is bare, and no file ends inside a block. Text this plan appends to a file is the content of a code block, never the fence lines around it; Tasks 4 and 6 run a check that prints each file's state.
- No simulation inside the pipeline (D-016). No CI (D-005): `scripts/verify.ps1` passes locally before every merge to `main`.
- Commits: `type(scope): summary` in lower case, no agent, model, or tool attribution and no `Co-Authored-By` trailers (D-001, D-002); author identity per D-050.
- **Pushes (D-098).** Slices are rebased onto local `main` and nothing is pushed while the stage runs. After the close-out (Task 6), its tag, and a fresh clone of `v0.8.5` that passes `scripts/verify.ps1`, the controller pushes `main` and `v0.8.5` (Task 7); never a force push.
- `.gh` example definitions are made and saved by a person in Rhino 8; agents never write or check them (D-058). S8.6 adds none.
- S8.6 changes these earlier files: `PlanAssertions.cs` (S8.1, unchanged since), `StairBayLayout.cs` (S8.1, last changed in S8.3), `TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1, last changed in S8.5), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1, last changed in S8.5), and `Directory.Build.props` (S0, last changed in S8.5); and the documents AGENTS.md, README.md, ADR-006, ADR-008, ADR-002, `pipeline.md`, `program-presets.md`, `syn-typologies.md`, the roadmap, `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md`, and the decision log (all last changed in S8.5).

By default a rectangle of 48 m × 21.8 m (1046.4 m²): `SUP` 261.6 m², `DC1` and `DC2` 86.4 m², `TB1` and `TB2` 252 m², `CC1` 108 m²; the outdoor walls are `SUP`'s west, south, and north sides, every band's east end, `DC1`'s south side, and `DC2`'s north side; 8 windows with 71.988 m² of glazing (three on `SUP`, two on each dirty corridor, one at the clean corridor's end), none on a bank and no diagnostic.

- [x] **Step 2: Make the window check state the WWR-0 rule — replace `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/PlanAssertions.cs` (full file)**

`HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall` looks up the zone's WWR before it checks the wall, and treats a wall whose zone's preset has WWR 0 like a wall that is not outdoor: it must have no window. Nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests;

/// <summary>Checks every plan generator must pass (ADR-006): tiling, the D-026 window rule, and adjacency queries.</summary>
internal static class PlanAssertions
{
    /// <summary>The zones cover the footprint's area and no two zones overlap.</summary>
    public static void TilesTheFootprint(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        var ops = new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default);
        Assert.True(ToleranceSettings.Default.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, plan.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea)));
        for (int i = 0; i < plan.Zones.Count; i++)
        {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < plan.Zones.Count; j++)
            {
                Assert.Equal(0.0, ops.IntersectionArea(plan.Zones[i].Parts[0].Footprint, plan.Zones[j].Parts[0].Footprint), 9);
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Every outdoor wall has one window centred on it with area WWR × wall area of its zone's preset (D-026), except that a zone whose preset has
    /// WWR 0 has no window (ADR-011, rule 1); other walls have none.
    /// </summary>
    public static void HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(IGeneratedPlan plan, PlanPresets presets)
    {
        foreach (WallSurface wall in plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>())
        {
            Zone zone = plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id == wall.Zone);
            double ratio = presets.Slots.Single(s => s.SpaceType == zone.SpaceType).Preset.WindowToWallRatio;
            if (wall.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Outdoors || ratio == 0.0)
            {
                Assert.Empty(wall.Windows);
                continue;
            }

            Window window = Assert.Single(wall.Windows);
            Assert.Equal(ratio * wall.Area, window.Area, 9);
            Assert.Equal((wall.Length - window.Width) / 2.0, window.Offset, 12);
            Assert.Equal((wall.Height - window.Height) / 2.0, window.SillHeight, 12);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>IDs of the zones that share an interzone wall with a zone, in ordinal order.</summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<string> Neighbours(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) =>
        plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Zone.Value == zone && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone)
            .Select(w => w.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value)
            .Distinct()
            .OrderBy(id => id, System.StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .ToArray();

    /// <summary>Plan orientation bins of a zone's outdoor walls, in ordinal order of their names.</summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<OrientationBin> OutdoorFacings(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) =>
        plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Zone.Value == zone && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors)
            .Select(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth))
            .Distinct()
            .OrderBy(b => b.ToString(), System.StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .ToArray();
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OperatingSuite/OperatingSuiteGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

36 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plan (zone IDs, names, space types, rectangles, areas); the band order for 2 to 5 banks; tiling for five parameter sets; every band adjoining `SUP` over its full depth (R001, 2 to 5 banks); every bank between exactly one clean and one dirty corridor and no other bank, and every corridor adjoining only banks and `SUP` (R002, 2 to 5 banks); the corridors shared by consecutive banks; the outdoor facings; the windows and glazing, none on the banks and no diagnostic; a glazed theatre preset (WWR 0.2) giving 10 windows; a bank count of 1; invalid lengths; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.OperatingSuite;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.OperatingSuite;

public sealed class OperatingSuiteGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly OperatingSuitePresets Examples = new(
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.OperatingTheatre,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CleanCorridor,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.DirtyCorridor,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ClinicalSupport);

    private readonly OperatingSuiteGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new OperatingSuiteParameters(
                BankCount: 2,
                BankLength: 36.0,
                TheatreDepth: 7.0,
                CleanCorridorWidth: 3.0,
                DirtyCorridorWidth: 2.4,
                SupportLength: 12.0,
                FloorHeight: 4.2,
                OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            OperatingSuiteParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsASupportZoneBesideBandsOfBanksAndCorridors()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "SUP", "DC1", "TB1", "CC1", "TB2", "DC2" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Support", "Dirty Corridor 1", "Theatre Bank 1", "Clean Corridor 1", "Theatre Bank 2", "Dirty Corridor 2" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { SpaceType.ClinicalSupport, SpaceType.DirtyCorridor, SpaceType.OperatingTheatre, SpaceType.CleanCorridor, SpaceType.OperatingTheatre, SpaceType.DirtyCorridor },
            plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(48.0 * 21.8, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        double[] areas = { 12.0 * 21.8, 36.0 * 2.4, 36.0 * 7.0, 36.0 * 3.0, 36.0 * 7.0, 36.0 * 2.4 };
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Zip(areas, (z, a) => (z.FloorArea, a)), p => Assert.Equal(p.a, p.FloorArea, 9));
        AssertRectangle(0, 0, 12, 21.8, Outer(plan, "SUP"));
        AssertRectangle(12, 0, 48, 2.4, Outer(plan, "DC1"));
        AssertRectangle(12, 2.4, 48, 9.4, Outer(plan, "TB1"));
        AssertRectangle(12, 9.4, 48, 12.4, Outer(plan, "CC1"));
        AssertRectangle(12, 12.4, 48, 19.4, Outer(plan, "TB2"));
        AssertRectangle(12, 19.4, 48, 21.8, Outer(plan, "DC2"));
        AssertRectangle(0, 0, 48, 21.8, plan.Footprint.Outer.ToArray());
        Assert.Empty(plan.Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(4.2, z.Parts[0].Height));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2, new[] { "SUP", "DC1", "TB1", "CC1", "TB2", "DC2" })]
    [InlineData(3, new[] { "SUP", "DC1", "TB1", "CC1", "TB2", "DC2", "TB3", "CC2" })]
    [InlineData(4, new[] { "SUP", "DC1", "TB1", "CC1", "TB2", "DC2", "TB3", "CC2", "TB4", "DC3" })]
    [InlineData(5, new[] { "SUP", "DC1", "TB1", "CC1", "TB2", "DC2", "TB3", "CC2", "TB4", "DC3", "TB5", "CC3" })]
    public void BandsAlternateFromADirtyCorridorInTheSouth(int banks, string[] zones)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = banks });

        Assert.Equal(zones, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Skip(1).OrderBy(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.Min(p => p.Y)).Select(z => z.Id.Value), plan.Zones.Skip(1).Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        int clean = (banks + 1) / 2;
        int dirty = banks + 1 - clean;
        Assert.Equal((banks * 7.0) + (clean * 3.0) + (dirty * 2.4), plan.Footprint.Outer.Max(p => p.Y), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2, 36.0, 7.0, 3.0, 2.4, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 36.0, 7.0, 3.0, 2.4, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(5, 36.0, 7.0, 3.0, 2.4, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(2, 20.5, 6.3, 2.2, 1.8, 7.7)]
    [InlineData(4, 50.0, 9.0, 4.0, 3.0, 20.0)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int banks, double length, double theatre, double clean, double dirty, double support)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(new OperatingSuiteParameters(banks, length, theatre, clean, dirty, support, 4.2, 0.0));

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        int cleanCount = (banks + 1) / 2;
        double depth = (banks * theatre) + (cleanCount * clean) + ((banks + 1 - cleanCount) * dirty);
        Assert.Equal((support + length) * depth, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal((2 * banks) + 2, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    [InlineData(5)]
    public void EveryBankAndCorridorAdjoinsTheSupportRooms(int banks)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = banks });
        string[] bands = plan.Zones.Skip(1).Select(z => z.Id.Value).ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(bands.OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal), PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "SUP"));
        foreach (string band in bands)
        {
            WallSurface root = Assert.Single(plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == band && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == "SUP");
            Assert.Equal(Depth(plan, band), root.Length, 9);
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    [InlineData(5)]
    public void EveryBankLiesBetweenOneCleanAndOneDirtyCorridorAndTheRoutesNeverMeet(int banks)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = banks });
        Dictionary<string, SpaceType> types = plan.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id.Value, z => z.SpaceType);

        foreach (Zone bank in plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.OperatingTheatre))
        {
            IReadOnlyList<string> neighbours = PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, bank.Id.Value);
            Assert.Single(neighbours, n => types[n] == SpaceType.CleanCorridor);
            Assert.Single(neighbours, n => types[n] == SpaceType.DirtyCorridor);
            Assert.DoesNotContain(neighbours, n => types[n] == SpaceType.OperatingTheatre);
            Assert.All(
                plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone == bank.Id && w.AdjacentZone is { } other && types[other.Value] != SpaceType.ClinicalSupport),
                w => Assert.Equal(36.0, w.Length, 9));
        }

        foreach (Zone corridor in plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType is SpaceType.CleanCorridor or SpaceType.DirtyCorridor))
        {
            Assert.All(PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, corridor.Id.Value), n => Assert.Contains(types[n], new[] { SpaceType.OperatingTheatre, SpaceType.ClinicalSupport }));
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ConsecutiveBanksShareTheCorridorBetweenThem()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = 3 });

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "CC1", "DC1", "SUP" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "TB1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "CC1", "DC2", "SUP" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "TB2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "CC2", "DC2", "SUP" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "TB3"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "SUP", "TB1", "TB2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "CC1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "SUP", "TB3" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "CC2"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnlyTheSupportsWestEndTheBandsEastEndsAndTheOuterBandsAreOutdoor()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "SUP"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "DC1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "TB1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "CC1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "TB2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "DC2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = 3 }), "CC2"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPresetAndTheTheatresNone()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default, Examples);

        // The theatre preset's WWR is 0, so the banks' east ends get no window and no WindowOmitted info (ADR-011 rule 1).
        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        WallSurface[] walls = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.All(walls.Where(w => w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("TB", StringComparison.Ordinal)), w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(8, walls.Sum(w => w.Windows.Count));
        double support = 0.2 * (21.8 + 12.0 + 12.0) * 4.2;
        double dirty = 2 * 0.1 * (36.0 + 2.4) * 4.2;
        double clean = 0.1 * 3.0 * 4.2;
        Assert.Equal(support + dirty + clean, walls.Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ATheatrePresetWithGlazingGivesTheBanksEastWindows()
    {
        ProgramPreset glazed = (ExampleNonResidentialPresets.OperatingTheatreValues with { WindowToWallRatio = 0.2 }).ToPreset().Value;
        OperatingSuitePresets presets = Examples with { OperatingTheatre = glazed };

        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default, presets);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, presets);
        Assert.Equal(10, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.Windows.Count));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ABankCountBelowTwoIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = 1 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal("BankCount must be at least 2 (the theatre banks repeat beside the support rooms), got 1.", error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 7.0, 3.0, 2.4, 12.0, "BankLength")]
    [InlineData(36.0, -7.0, 3.0, 2.4, 12.0, "TheatreDepth")]
    [InlineData(36.0, 7.0, double.NaN, 2.4, 12.0, "CleanCorridorWidth")]
    [InlineData(36.0, 7.0, 3.0, 0.0, 12.0, "DirtyCorridorWidth")]
    [InlineData(36.0, 7.0, 3.0, 2.4, -12.0, "SupportLength")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 7.0, 3.0, 2.4, 12.0, "BankLength")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double length, double theatre, double clean, double dirty, double support, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new OperatingSuiteParameters(2, length, theatre, clean, dirty, support, 4.2, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new OperatingSuiteParameters(0, 0.0, -7.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(8, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                ("OperatingTheatre", SpaceType.OperatingTheatre), ("CleanCorridor", SpaceType.CleanCorridor), ("DirtyCorridor", SpaceType.DirtyCorridor),
                ("ClinicalSupport", SpaceType.ClinicalSupport),
            },
            Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.OperatingTheatre, Examples.CleanCorridor, Examples.DirtyCorridor, Examples.ClinicalSupport }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(
            OperatingSuiteParameters.Default,
            new OperatingSuitePresets(
                ExampleNonResidentialPresets.OperatingTheatre,
                ExampleNonResidentialPresets.DirtyCorridor,
                ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CleanCorridor,
                ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ClinicalSupport));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "CleanCorridor", "DirtyCorridor" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(OperatingSuiteGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "BankCount=2", "BankLength=36", "CleanCorridorWidth=3", "DirtyCorridorWidth=2.4", "FloorHeight=4.2", "OrientationDegrees=0",
                "Presets=OperatingTheatre:Example Operating Theatre;CleanCorridor:Example Clean Corridor;DirtyCorridor:Example Dirty Corridor;ClinicalSupport:Example Clinical Support",
                "SupportLength=12", "TheatreDepth=7",
            },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default)), "operating-suite-canonical");
    }

    private static void AssertRectangle(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY, Point2[] actual)
    {
        Point2[] expected = Polygon2.Rectangle(minX, minY, maxX, maxY).Outer.ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(expected.Length, actual.Length);
        for (int i = 0; i < expected.Length; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].X, actual[i].X, 9);
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].Y, actual[i].Y, 9);
        }
    }

    private static double Depth(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone)
    {
        Point2[] outer = Outer(plan, zone);
        return outer.Max(p => p.Y) - outer.Min(p => p.Y);
    }

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(OperatingSuiteParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 6: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/OperatingSuite/OperatingSuiteGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.OperatingSuite;

/// <summary>Parameters of the operating suite with separated clean and dirty routes (SYN-TYP-015, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="BankCount">Number of theatre banks; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="BankLength">Length of every bank and corridor along plan x, from the support rooms to the east façade, m.</param>
/// <param name="TheatreDepth">Depth of every theatre bank across the bands, m.</param>
/// <param name="CleanCorridorWidth">Width of every clean corridor, m.</param>
/// <param name="DirtyCorridorWidth">Width of every dirty corridor, m.</param>
/// <param name="SupportLength">Length of the support rooms along plan x at the suite's west end, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record OperatingSuiteParameters(
    int BankCount,
    double BankLength,
    double TheatreDepth,
    double CleanCorridorWidth,
    double DirtyCorridorWidth,
    double SupportLength,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The ADR-006 defaults: 2 banks of 36 m × 7 m, a 3 m clean and 2.4 m dirty corridors, 12 m of support rooms, 4.2 m storeys, orientation 0°.
    /// </summary>
    public static OperatingSuiteParameters Default { get; } = new(2, 36.0, 7.0, 3.0, 2.4, 12.0, 4.2, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the operating suite, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="OperatingTheatre">Preset of the theatre banks; space type <see cref="SpaceType.OperatingTheatre"/>.</param>
/// <param name="CleanCorridor">Preset of the clean corridors; space type <see cref="SpaceType.CleanCorridor"/>.</param>
/// <param name="DirtyCorridor">Preset of the dirty corridors; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DirtyCorridor"/>.</param>
/// <param name="ClinicalSupport">Preset of the support rooms; space type <see cref="SpaceType.ClinicalSupport"/>.</param>
public sealed record OperatingSuitePresets(ProgramPreset OperatingTheatre, ProgramPreset CleanCorridor, ProgramPreset DirtyCorridor, ProgramPreset ClinicalSupport)
    : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(OperatingTheatre), SpaceType.OperatingTheatre, OperatingTheatre),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(CleanCorridor), SpaceType.CleanCorridor, CleanCorridor),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DirtyCorridor), SpaceType.DirtyCorridor, DirtyCorridor),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(ClinicalSupport), SpaceType.ClinicalSupport, ClinicalSupport),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Operating suite with separated clean and dirty routes (SYN-TYP-015, ADR-006): repeated theatre banks beside the support rooms, every bank and
/// corridor adjoining them (R001), and distinct clean and dirty routes (R002): every bank lies between one clean and one dirty corridor, and no clean
/// corridor adjoins a dirty one. Zones are departments (D-097, ADR-014): each theatre bank, each corridor, and the support rooms together.
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// The support rooms <c>SUP</c> span the suite's full depth at its west end, <c>[0, SupportLength]</c>; east of them, bands of length
/// <c>BankLength</c> along plan x are stacked from south to north: a dirty corridor, then for each bank the bank and the next corridor, the corridors
/// alternating clean and dirty, so consecutive banks share a corridor. Zone IDs: <c>SUP</c>, then the bands south to north, <c>DC1</c>,
/// <c>TB1</c>, <c>CC1</c>, <c>TB2</c>, <c>DC2</c>, <c>TB3</c>, <c>CC2</c>, …; display names <c>Support</c>, <c>Dirty Corridor 1</c>,
/// <c>Theatre Bank 1</c>, <c>Clean Corridor 1</c>. Only the support rooms' west, south, and north façades, the bands' east ends, and the outer
/// side of the southmost and northmost corridors are outdoor walls; a bank's only façade is its east end, which the theatre preset's WWR of 0 leaves
/// without a window.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class OperatingSuiteGenerator : PlanGenerator<OperatingSuiteParameters, OperatingSuitePresets>
{
    /// <summary>The fewest theatre banks: the family repeats them.</summary>
    public const int MinimumBanks = 2;

    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public OperatingSuiteGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(OperatingSuiteGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(OperatingSuiteParameters parameters)
    {
        if (parameters.BankCount < MinimumBanks)
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(OperatingSuiteParameters.BankCount)} must be at least {MinimumBanks} (the theatre banks repeat beside the support rooms), got {parameters.BankCount}.");
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(OperatingSuiteParameters.BankLength), parameters.BankLength),
            (nameof(OperatingSuiteParameters.TheatreDepth), parameters.TheatreDepth),
            (nameof(OperatingSuiteParameters.CleanCorridorWidth), parameters.CleanCorridorWidth),
            (nameof(OperatingSuiteParameters.DirtyCorridorWidth), parameters.DirtyCorridorWidth),
            (nameof(OperatingSuiteParameters.SupportLength), parameters.SupportLength),
        };
        foreach ((string name, double value) in values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)))
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{name} must be positive, got {value}.");
        }
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(OperatingSuiteParameters p)
    {
        double west = p.SupportLength;
        double east = p.SupportLength + p.BankLength;
        var bands = new List<PlannedZone>();
        double y = 0.0;
        int clean = 0;
        int dirty = 0;
        for (int corridor = 0; corridor <= p.BankCount; corridor++)
        {
            if (corridor > 0)
            {
                double top = y + p.TheatreDepth;
                bands.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"TB{corridor}"), $"Theatre Bank {corridor}", SpaceType.OperatingTheatre, Polygon2.Rectangle(west, y, east, top)));
                y = top;
            }

            // Corridors alternate from a dirty corridor in the south, so every bank lies between one clean and one dirty corridor.
            bool isClean = corridor % 2 == 1;
            double next = y + (isClean ? p.CleanCorridorWidth : p.DirtyCorridorWidth);
            Polygon2 shape = Polygon2.Rectangle(west, y, east, next);
            if (isClean)
            {
                clean++;
                bands.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"CC{clean}"), $"Clean Corridor {clean}", SpaceType.CleanCorridor, shape));
            }
            else
            {
                dirty++;
                bands.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"DC{dirty}"), $"Dirty Corridor {dirty}", SpaceType.DirtyCorridor, shape));
            }

            y = next;
        }

        var zones = new List<PlannedZone> { new(new ZoneId("SUP"), "Support", SpaceType.ClinicalSupport, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, west, y)) };
        zones.AddRange(bands);
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, east, y), zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(OperatingSuiteParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.BankCount), p.BankCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.BankLength), Provenance.Format(p.BankLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.TheatreDepth), Provenance.Format(p.TheatreDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CleanCorridorWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CleanCorridorWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.DirtyCorridorWidth), Provenance.Format(p.DirtyCorridorWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.SupportLength), Provenance.Format(p.SupportLength)),
    };
}
```

- [x] **Step 10: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/OperatingSuiteTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

99 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32, rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m, and rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostBanks` (32, 2 and 5 banks), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheMostBanksOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (16, 5 banks at 131.4°), `SemanticMergeKeepsEveryDepartmentBecauseNoTwoOfOneTypeTouch` (2 and 5 banks), and `PerimeterCoreKeepsTheBandsEastWindowsOnPerimeterEast` (`P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, `CORE`; `P-East` carries all 3.276 m² of east glazing; `CORE` from `CC1`, `SUP`, `TB1`, and `TB2`). No production code changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.OperatingSuite;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.6 operating suite (SYN-TYP-015, ADR-006): theatre banks between alternating clean and dirty corridors beside the support rooms, through
/// every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most tested banks.
/// </summary>
public sealed class OperatingSuiteTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>The fewest banks (ADR-006) and the most tested; the family has no upper bound.</summary>
    private static readonly int[] Counts = { OperatingSuiteGenerator.MinimumBanks, 5 };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Counts
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostBanks(string simplifier, string aggregator, int banks)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = banks }), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheMostBanksOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[1];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = Counts[1] }), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(5)]
    public void SemanticMergeKeepsEveryDepartmentBecauseNoTwoOfOneTypeTouch(int banks)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(OperatingSuiteParameters.Default with { BankCount = banks });

        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType), floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value), floor.Zones.Select(z => Assert.Single(z.SourceZones).Value));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreKeepsTheBandsEastWindowsOnPerimeterEast()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan();

        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal("P-North,P-East,P-South,P-West,CORE", string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        double east = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.East).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea);
        Assert.Equal(0.1 * (2.4 + 3.0 + 2.4) * 4.2, east, 9);
        Assert.Equal(east, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "P-East").Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "CC1", "SUP", "TB1", "TB2" },
            floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").SourceZones.Select(s => s.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal));
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static OperatingSuitePresets Presets() => new(
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.OperatingTheatre,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CleanCorridor,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.DirtyCorridor,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ClinicalSupport);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(OperatingSuiteParameters? parameters = null) =>
        new OperatingSuiteGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters ?? OperatingSuiteParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

### Task 2: Linked housing clusters around several courts (`SYN-TYP-011`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs` (S8.1 file, last changed in S8.3; summary only)
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/CourtCluster/CourtClusterGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/CourtCluster/CourtClusterGeneratorTests.cs`, `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/CourtClusterTypologyTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/CourtCluster/Snapshots/court-cluster-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: as Task 1, with `Polygon2.Create` (an outer ring and holes), `StairBayLayout.Wing`, `Fit`, and `FitErrors` (S8.1–S8.3, internal), `SpaceType.DwellingUnit` and `Stair`, `ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit` and `Stair`, `PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default)` in the tests, and `SingleZoneBuilding` in the integration tests; *Perimeter Core* on footprints with holes (S8.3, ADR-008).
- Produces: `public sealed record CourtClusterParameters(int CourtCount, double CourtWidth, double CourtDepth, double Depth, double UnitWidth, double StairWidth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (2, 24, 24, 12, 7, 3, 3, 0); `public sealed record CourtClusterPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets` (slots `DwellingUnit`, `Stair`); `public sealed class CourtClusterGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `CourtClusterGenerator`, `public const int MinimumCourts = 2`: with depth `D`, court width `w`, court depth `c`, and `k` courts, the plan is `(k + 1)·D + k·w` wide and `2D + c` deep; the south and north wings span the full width, the west wing, the middle wings `i` at `x = i·(D + w)` (*Middle i*, IDs `M{i}-…`), and the east wing at `x = k·(D + w)` lie between them over `[D, D + c]`; court `j` is `[(j − 1)(D + w) + D, j(D + w)] × [D, D + c]`, a clockwise hole, west to east; every wing is filled with stair bays by `StairBayLayout` as in `EnclosedCourtGenerator`, which is not changed (D-104); errors `InvalidParameter` for fewer than 2 courts (`CourtCount must be at least 2 (one court is SYN-TYP-005, Enclosed Court), got 1.`), for each length that is not positive and finite, and `FitErrors` for `each {W} m south and north wing` (only when the count is valid) and `each {c} m west, middle, and east wing`; no upper bound; provenance keys `CourtCount`, `CourtWidth`, `CourtDepth`, `Depth`, `UnitWidth`, `StairWidth`. By default 84 m × 48 m less two courts (2880 m²), 39 zones: the long wings five bays of 6.9 m dwellings, the side wings one bay of 10.5 m dwellings; 72 windows with 374.4 m² of glazing, 32 of them on the court façades (158.4 m², 16 per court); the shortest outdoor walls are 2.4 m court-edge fragments, all with windows; no diagnostic.

- [x] **Step 1: Name the court cluster in the stair bay's summary — replace `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs` (full file)**

The summary lists the court cluster (`SYN-TYP-011`) among the users of the stair bay; no code changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

/// <summary>
/// The stair bay of ADR-006: dwelling | stair | dwelling side by side along a bar, each through the full depth, the stair serving the two dwellings
/// beside it. Shared by <see cref="StairBayBarGenerator"/> (HDHM-TYP-001), the stair pair (HDHM-TYP-008), and the wings of the winged band
/// (HDHM-TYP-003), the open court (HDHM-TYP-004), the enclosed court (HDHM-TYP-005), and the court cluster (SYN-TYP-011), so the bay geometry
/// exists once.
/// </summary>
internal static class StairBayLayout
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays west to east from the origin. Zones: <c>ST1…STn</c> (stairs, west to east), then <c>U1…U2n</c>
    /// (dwellings, west to east); bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Bar depth, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The footprint and the zones.</returns>
    public static PlanLayout Bays(int bays, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double length = bays * ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth);
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, length, depth), Wing(string.Empty, string.Empty, 0.0, 0.0, length, depth, alongY: false, bays, unitWidth, stairWidth));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The stair bays that fill a wing of length <paramref name="length"/> exactly (ADR-006): <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays, rounded
    /// half away from zero, with the dwelling width adjusted to <c>(L − n·S) / (2n)</c>, which is not positive when the stairs leave no room.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Target dwelling width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <returns>The bay count and the adjusted dwelling width.</returns>
    public static (int Bays, double UnitWidth) Fit(double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double bays = Math.Max(1.0, Math.Round(length / ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth), MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
        int count = bays >= int.MaxValue ? int.MaxValue : (int)bays;
        return (count, (length - (count * stairWidth)) / (2.0 * count));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The error for a wing whose fitted stair bays leave no dwelling width (<see cref="Fit"/>), or none; the message names the wing, such as
    /// <c>each 17 m wing</c>, and the stairs it would need.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="wing">The wing as named in the message.</param>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length, m.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Target dwelling width, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or one <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter"/> error.</returns>
    public static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> FitErrors(string wing, double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth)
    {
        (int bays, double width) = Fit(length, unitWidth, stairWidth);
        if (width > 0)
        {
            yield break;
        }

        string need = bays == 1
            ? $"its one stair bay needs {Provenance.Format(stairWidth)} m of stairs"
            : $"its {bays} stair bays need {Provenance.Format(bays * stairWidth)} m of stairs";
        yield return Diagnostic.Error(
            DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
            $"{nameof(StairBayBarParameters.StairWidth)} {Provenance.Format(stairWidth)} m leaves no dwelling width in {wing}: {need}; "
            + $"reduce {nameof(StairBayBarParameters.StairWidth)} or {nameof(StairBayBarParameters.UnitWidth)}.");
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays along a wing that starts at (<paramref name="x0"/>, <paramref name="y0"/>) and runs along +x, or
    /// along +y when <paramref name="alongY"/>; the last bay ends exactly at the wing's length. Zones: <c>{prefix}ST1…</c>, then <c>{prefix}U1…</c>,
    /// numbered along the wing's axis; bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="prefix">Zone ID prefix, such as <c>B-</c>.</param>
    /// <param name="namePrefix">Display name prefix, such as <c>Band </c>.</param>
    /// <param name="x0">Wing start, plan x, m.</param>
    /// <param name="y0">Wing start, plan y, m.</param>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length along its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Wing depth across its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="alongY">Whether the wing runs along +y instead of +x.</param>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The stairs, then the dwellings.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Wing(
        string prefix,
        string namePrefix,
        double x0,
        double y0,
        double length,
        double depth,
        bool alongY,
        int bays,
        double unitWidth,
        double stairWidth)
    {
        double bayWidth = (2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth;
        double start = alongY ? y0 : x0;
        Polygon2 Piece(double from, double to) => alongY ? Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, from, x0 + depth, to) : Polygon2.Rectangle(from, y0, to, y0 + depth);
        var stairs = new List<PlannedZone>();
        var units = new List<PlannedZone>();
        for (int i = 0; i < bays; i++)
        {
            double west = start + (i * bayWidth);
            double stairWest = west + unitWidth;
            double stairEast = stairWest + stairWidth;
            double east = i == bays - 1 ? start + length : west + bayWidth;
            stairs.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}ST{i + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Stair {i + 1}", SpaceType.Stair, Piece(stairWest, stairEast)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(west, stairWest)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 2}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 2}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(stairEast, east)));
        }

        stairs.AddRange(units);
        return stairs;
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/CourtCluster/CourtClusterGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

31 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical zones, names, areas, and rectangles; the footprint with two holes (exact rings) and no overlap with either court; tiling and the hole count for 2, 3, 4, and 6 courts and odd dimensions; every middle wing with only court façades, 24 m into each of its two courts (2 to 4 courts); every court with 24 m of façade per side from the right wings, each wall with a window (2 and 3 courts); a stair in every wing and every dwelling adjoining one; the windows (72, 32 on the courts); a court count of 1; invalid lengths; the stair-fit errors (exact messages); every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.CourtCluster;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.CourtCluster;

public sealed class CourtClusterGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly CourtClusterPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private static readonly string[] CanonicalZones =
    {
        "S-ST1", "S-ST2", "S-ST3", "S-ST4", "S-ST5", "S-U1", "S-U2", "S-U3", "S-U4", "S-U5", "S-U6", "S-U7", "S-U8", "S-U9", "S-U10",
        "N-ST1", "N-ST2", "N-ST3", "N-ST4", "N-ST5", "N-U1", "N-U2", "N-U3", "N-U4", "N-U5", "N-U6", "N-U7", "N-U8", "N-U9", "N-U10",
        "W-ST1", "W-U1", "W-U2", "M1-ST1", "M1-U1", "M1-U2", "E-ST1", "E-U1", "E-U2",
    };

    private readonly CourtClusterGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new CourtClusterParameters(CourtCount: 2, CourtWidth: 24.0, CourtDepth: 24.0, Depth: 12.0, UnitWidth: 7.0, StairWidth: 3.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            CourtClusterParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsFiveStairBayWingsAroundTwoCourts()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(CanonicalZones, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal((84.0 * 48.0) - (2 * 24.0 * 24.0), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "South Stair 2", "North Unit 3", "West Unit 2", "Middle 1 Stair 1", "Middle 1 Unit 2", "East Stair 1" },
            plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value is "S-ST2" or "N-U3" or "W-U2" or "M1-ST1" or "M1-U2" or "E-ST1").Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair), z => Assert.Equal(36.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("S-U", StringComparison.Ordinal) || z.Id.Value.StartsWith("N-U", StringComparison.Ordinal)), z => Assert.Equal(6.9 * 12.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value is "W-U1" or "W-U2" or "M1-U1" or "M1-U2" or "E-U1" or "E-U2"), z => Assert.Equal(10.5 * 12.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        AssertRectangle(23.7, 0.0, 26.7, 12.0, Outer(plan, "S-ST2"));
        AssertRectangle(16.8, 36.0, 23.7, 48.0, Outer(plan, "N-U3"));
        AssertRectangle(0.0, 22.5, 12.0, 25.5, Outer(plan, "W-ST1"));
        AssertRectangle(36.0, 12.0, 48.0, 22.5, Outer(plan, "M1-U1"));
        AssertRectangle(36.0, 22.5, 48.0, 25.5, Outer(plan, "M1-ST1"));
        AssertRectangle(72.0, 25.5, 84.0, 36.0, Outer(plan, "E-U2"));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(3.0, z.Parts[0].Height));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheFootprintIsARectangleWithOneHolePerCourt()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(84, 0), new Point2(84, 48), new Point2(0, 48) }, plan.Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.Equal(2, plan.Footprint.Holes.Count);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(12, 12), new Point2(12, 36), new Point2(36, 36), new Point2(36, 12) }, plan.Footprint.Holes[0]);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(48, 12), new Point2(48, 36), new Point2(72, 36), new Point2(72, 12) }, plan.Footprint.Holes[1]);
        var ops = new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, ops.IntersectionArea(plan.Footprint, Polygon2.Rectangle(12, 12, 36, 36)), 9);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, ops.IntersectionArea(plan.Footprint, Polygon2.Rectangle(48, 12, 72, 36)), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2, 24.0, 24.0, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 24.0, 24.0, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(4, 24.0, 24.0, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(6, 24.0, 24.0, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(2, 6.0, 40.0, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 50.0, 4.0, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(4, 17.5, 30.5, 9.0)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int courts, double courtWidth, double courtDepth, double depth)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = courts, CourtWidth = courtWidth, CourtDepth = courtDepth, Depth = depth });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(courts, plan.Footprint.Holes.Count);
        double width = ((courts + 1) * depth) + (courts * courtWidth);
        Assert.Equal((width * ((2 * depth) + courtDepth)) - (courts * courtWidth * courtDepth), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        string[] wings = new[] { "S", "N", "W" }.Concat(Enumerable.Range(1, courts - 1).Select(i => $"M{i}")).Append("E").ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(wings, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value.Split('-')[0]).Distinct());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    public void EveryMiddleWingBordersTwoCourts(int courts)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = courts });

        for (int i = 1; i < courts; i++)
        {
            string prefix = $"M{i}-";
            Zone[] wing = plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith(prefix, StringComparison.Ordinal)).ToArray();
            Assert.Equal(new[] { $"{prefix}ST1", $"{prefix}U1", $"{prefix}U2" }, wing.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
            Assert.All(wing, z => Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, z.Id.Value)));
            WallSurface[] facades = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Zone.Value.StartsWith(prefix, StringComparison.Ordinal)).ToArray();
            Assert.Equal(24.0, facades.Where(w => OnCourt(w, i)).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
            Assert.Equal(24.0, facades.Where(w => OnCourt(w, i + 1)).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
            Assert.Equal(facades.Length, facades.Count(w => OnCourt(w, i) || OnCourt(w, i + 1)));
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    public void EveryCourtIsClosedByDwellingsWithWindowsFacingIntoIt(int courts)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = courts });
        WallSurface[] outdoor = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();

        for (int court = 1; court <= courts; court++)
        {
            WallSurface[] facades = outdoor.Where(w => OnCourt(w, court)).ToArray();
            Assert.Equal(24.0, Facing(facades, OrientationBin.North).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
            Assert.Equal(24.0, Facing(facades, OrientationBin.South).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
            Assert.Equal(24.0, Facing(facades, OrientationBin.East).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
            Assert.Equal(24.0, Facing(facades, OrientationBin.West).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
            Assert.All(Facing(facades, OrientationBin.North), w => Assert.StartsWith("S-", w.Zone.Value, StringComparison.Ordinal));
            Assert.All(Facing(facades, OrientationBin.South), w => Assert.StartsWith("N-", w.Zone.Value, StringComparison.Ordinal));
            Assert.All(Facing(facades, OrientationBin.East), w => Assert.StartsWith(court == 1 ? "W-" : $"M{court - 1}-", w.Zone.Value, StringComparison.Ordinal));
            Assert.All(Facing(facades, OrientationBin.West), w => Assert.StartsWith(court == courts ? "E-" : $"M{court}-", w.Zone.Value, StringComparison.Ordinal));
            Assert.All(facades, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryWingHasItsOwnStairsAndEveryDwellingAdjoinsAStair()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = 3 });

        Assert.All(
            plan.Zones.GroupBy(z => z.Id.Value.Split('-')[0]),
            wing => Assert.Contains(wing, z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair));
        Assert.All(
            plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit),
            z => Assert.Contains(PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, z.Id.Value), n => plan.Zones.Single(s => s.Id.Value == n).SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        WallSurface[] walls = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(72, walls.Sum(w => w.Windows.Count));
        Assert.Equal(32, walls.Where(w => OnCourt(w, 1) || OnCourt(w, 2)).Sum(w => w.Windows.Count));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ACourtCountBelowTwoIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = 1 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal("CourtCount must be at least 2 (one court is SYN-TYP-005, Enclosed Court), got 1.", error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtWidth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, -24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtDepth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 24.0, 0.0, 7.0, 3.0, "Depth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 24.0, 12.0, double.NaN, 3.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, -3.0, "StairWidth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtWidth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double courtWidth, double courtDepth, double depth, double unitWidth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new CourtClusterParameters(2, courtWidth, courtDepth, depth, unitWidth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StairsThatLeaveNoRoomForDwellingsAreAnError()
    {
        // 24 m west, middle, and east wings with 30 m stairs: one bay needs more than the wing's length; the 84 m south and north wings
        // (round(84 / 31) = 3 bays, 90 m of stairs) do not fit either.
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { UnitWidth = 0.5, StairWidth = 30.0 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "StairWidth 30 m leaves no dwelling width in each 84 m south and north wing: its 3 stair bays need 90 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.",
                "StairWidth 30 m leaves no dwelling width in each 24 m west, middle, and east wing: its one stair bay needs 30 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.",
            },
            result.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.Message));
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, d.Code));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new CourtClusterParameters(0, 0.0, 0.0, -12.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(8, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default, new CourtClusterPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(CourtClusterGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "CourtCount=2", "CourtDepth=24", "CourtWidth=24", "Depth=12", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0",
                "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=3", "UnitWidth=7",
            },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(CourtClusterParameters.Default)), "court-cluster-canonical");
    }

    /// <summary>Whether a wall lies on the edge of court <paramref name="court"/> (from 1, west to east) of a plan with the default dimensions.</summary>
    private static bool OnCourt(WallSurface wall, int court)
    {
        double west = (court * 12.0) + ((court - 1) * 24.0);
        return new[] { wall.Start, wall.End }.All(p => p.X >= west - 1e-9 && p.X <= west + 24.0 + 1e-9 && p.Y is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0);
    }

    private static WallSurface[] Facing(IEnumerable<WallSurface> walls, OrientationBin bin) => walls.Where(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == bin).ToArray();

    private static void AssertRectangle(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY, Point2[] actual)
    {
        Point2[] expected = Polygon2.Rectangle(minX, minY, maxX, maxY).Outer.ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(expected.Length, actual.Length);
        for (int i = 0; i < expected.Length; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].X, actual[i].X, 9);
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].Y, actual[i].Y, 9);
        }
    }

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(CourtClusterParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```
