- [x] **Step 4: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/CourtCluster/CourtClusterGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

namespace Lod.Generators.CourtCluster;

/// <summary>Parameters of the linked housing clusters around several courts (SYN-TYP-011, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="CourtCount">Number of courts in a row along plan x; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="CourtWidth">Width of every court along plan x, between the wings on its west and east sides, m.</param>
/// <param name="CourtDepth">Depth of every court between the south and north wings, which is also the west, middle, and east wings' length, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Depth of every wing, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Target dwelling width along the wings, m; adjusted so the stair bays fill each wing exactly.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of each stair along the wings, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record CourtClusterParameters(
    int CourtCount,
    double CourtWidth,
    double CourtDepth,
    double Depth,
    double UnitWidth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 2 courts of 24 m × 24 m, 12 m deep wings, 7 m dwellings and 3 m stairs, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static CourtClusterParameters Default { get; } = new(2, 24.0, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the court cluster, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stairs; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record CourtClusterPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Linked housing clusters around several courts (SYN-TYP-011, ADR-006): a row of courts along plan x, closed by wings of stair bays, so the dwelling
/// wings are linked into one cluster around the courts (R001); every middle wing lies between two neighbouring courts, so the courts lie between
/// dwelling groups (R002); and every wing has its own stairs, so access is distributed (R003). The footprint is the outer rectangle with one hole per
/// court; each court is outside the building and its façades are outdoor walls facing into it. Each wing holds <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c>
/// bays of <see cref="StairBayLayout"/> with the dwelling width adjusted to fill its length <c>L</c>, as for the enclosed court (SYN-TYP-005). One
/// zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// With <c>D</c> the depth, <c>w</c> the court width, <c>c</c> the court depth, and <c>k</c> courts, the plan is <c>(k + 1)·D + k·w</c> wide: south
/// wing <c>[0, W] × [0, D]</c> and north wing <c>[0, W] × [D + c, 2D + c]</c> across the full width <c>W</c>; between them, along plan y over
/// <c>[D, D + c]</c>, the west wing at <c>x ∈ [0, D]</c>, middle wing <c>i</c> (from 1) at <c>x ∈ [i·(D + w), i·(D + w) + D]</c>, and the east
/// wing at <c>x ∈ [k·(D + w), k·(D + w) + D]</c>; court <c>j</c> (from 1) is <c>[(j − 1)·(D + w) + D, j·(D + w)] × [D, D + c]</c>, stored
/// clockwise, west to east. Zone IDs: <c>S-ST1…</c>, <c>S-U1…</c> and <c>N-…</c> (west to east), then <c>W-…</c>, <c>M1-…</c>, …, <c>E-…</c>
/// (south to north), each wing stairs first, then dwellings; display names <c>South Unit 3</c>, <c>Middle 1 Stair 1</c>. The generator does not
/// impose the precondition of <i>Perimeter Core</i> (D-087): its default 12 m wings are wider than twice the 4.57 m default perimeter depth.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class CourtClusterGenerator : PlanGenerator<CourtClusterParameters, CourtClusterPresets>
{
    /// <summary>The fewest courts: one court is the enclosed court (SYN-TYP-005).</summary>
    public const int MinimumCourts = 2;

    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public CourtClusterGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(CourtClusterGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(CourtClusterParameters parameters)
    {
        var errors = new List<Diagnostic>();
        bool counted = parameters.CourtCount >= MinimumCourts;
        if (!counted)
        {
            errors.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(CourtClusterParameters.CourtCount)} must be at least {MinimumCourts} (one court is SYN-TYP-005, Enclosed Court), got {parameters.CourtCount}."));
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(CourtClusterParameters.CourtWidth), parameters.CourtWidth),
            (nameof(CourtClusterParameters.CourtDepth), parameters.CourtDepth),
            (nameof(CourtClusterParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(CourtClusterParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(CourtClusterParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        var lengths = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToList();
        errors.AddRange(lengths);
        if (lengths.Count == 0)
        {
            // The south and north wings' length depends on the count, so their stair bays are checked only for a valid count.
            if (counted)
            {
                double width = Width(parameters);
                errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"each {Provenance.Format(width)} m south and north wing", width, parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
            }

            errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"each {Provenance.Format(parameters.CourtDepth)} m west, middle, and east wing", parameters.CourtDepth, parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(CourtClusterParameters p)
    {
        double d = p.Depth;
        double pitch = d + p.CourtWidth;
        double width = Width(p);
        double north = d + p.CourtDepth;
        (int longBays, double longUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(width, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        (int sideBays, double sideUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(p.CourtDepth, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>();
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("S-", "South ", 0.0, 0.0, width, d, alongY: false, longBays, longUnit, p.StairWidth));
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("N-", "North ", 0.0, north, width, d, alongY: false, longBays, longUnit, p.StairWidth));
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("W-", "West ", 0.0, d, p.CourtDepth, d, alongY: true, sideBays, sideUnit, p.StairWidth));
        for (int i = 1; i < p.CourtCount; i++)
        {
            zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing($"M{i}-", $"Middle {i} ", i * pitch, d, p.CourtDepth, d, alongY: true, sideBays, sideUnit, p.StairWidth));
        }

        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("E-", "East ", p.CourtCount * pitch, d, p.CourtDepth, d, alongY: true, sideBays, sideUnit, p.StairWidth));
        Point2[] outer = { new(0.0, 0.0), new(width, 0.0), new(width, north + d), new(0.0, north + d) };
        IEnumerable<Point2[]> courts = Enumerable.Range(0, p.CourtCount).Select(j =>
        {
            double west = (j * pitch) + d;
            double east = (j + 1) * pitch;
            return new[] { new Point2(west, d), new Point2(west, north), new Point2(east, north), new Point2(east, d) };
        });
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Create(outer, courts.ToArray()).Value, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(CourtClusterParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CourtCount), p.CourtCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CourtWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CourtWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CourtDepth), Provenance.Format(p.CourtDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };

    /// <summary>The plan's width along plan x: a wing on both sides of every court, <c>(k + 1)·D + k·w</c>.</summary>
    private static double Width(CourtClusterParameters p) => ((p.CourtCount + 1) * p.Depth) + (p.CourtCount * p.CourtWidth);
}
```

- [x] **Step 8: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/CourtClusterTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

102 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostCourts` (32, 2 and 4 courts), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheMostCourtsOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (16, 4 courts at 131.4°), `SemanticMergeJoinsTheDwellingsBetweenTheStairsAroundTheCourts` (6 dwelling groups of 374.4 m² and 457.2 m², 13 stairs, no holes), `PerimeterCoreGivesEveryCourtItsOwnCourtZonePerOrientation` (2, 3, and 4 courts: the zone IDs, court zones of 130.5649 m², as many core holes as courts, the core area, the court glazing conserved), `WingsNarrowerThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrow` (Depth 9: `PlanTooNarrow`), and `SingleZoneBuildingRebuildsEveryCourtsWallsOnEveryStorey` (two holes per part, 40 court walls each with a window, 2880 m² of ground). No production code changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.CourtCluster;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.6 court cluster (SYN-TYP-011, ADR-006): wings of stair bays around a row of courts, the first footprint with several holes, through every
/// simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most tested courts.
/// </summary>
public sealed class CourtClusterTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>The fewest courts (ADR-006) and the most tested; the family has no upper bound.</summary>
    private static readonly int[] Counts = { CourtClusterGenerator.MinimumCourts, 4 };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Counts
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostCourts(string simplifier, string aggregator, int courts)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = courts }), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheMostCourtsOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[1];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = Counts[1] }), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeJoinsTheDwellingsBetweenTheStairsAroundTheCourts()
    {
        // The middle stairs of the long wings and the stairs of the west, middle, and east wings separate the dwellings into groups: an L around each
        // outer corner and a T where a middle wing meets the south or north wing; stairs never adjoin each other and stay apart.
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Zone[] dwellings = floor.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(6, dwellings.Length);
        Assert.Equal(13, floor.Zones.Count(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 374.4, 457.2, 374.4, 374.4, 457.2, 374.4 }, dwellings.Select(z => Math.Round(z.FloorArea, 6)));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "M1-U1", "S-U4", "S-U5", "S-U6", "S-U7" },
            dwellings[1].SourceZones.Select(s => s.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    public void PerimeterCoreGivesEveryCourtItsOwnCourtZonePerOrientation(int courts)
    {
        // The trapezoids of every hole's edges are united per bin across the courts (ADR-008); the middle wings keep the courts' bands apart, so each
        // bin gives one polygon per court, numbered P-Court-<Bin>-1… in union output order.
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { CourtCount = courts });

        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(plan).Value;

        IEnumerable<string> court = new[] { "North", "East", "South", "West" }.SelectMany(b => Enumerable.Range(1, courts).Select(k => $"P-Court-{b}-{k}"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West" }.Concat(court).Append("CORE"), floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court", StringComparison.Ordinal)), z => Assert.Equal((24.0 + 4.57) * 4.57, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Polygon2 core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Equal(courts, core.Holes.Count);
        double width = ((courts + 1) * 12.0) + (courts * 24.0);
        Assert.Equal(((width - (2 * 4.57)) * (48.0 - (2 * 4.57))) - (courts * Math.Pow(24.0 + (2 * 4.57), 2)), core.Area, 6);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        double courtGlazing = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnAnyCourt(w, courts)).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea);
        Assert.Equal(courtGlazing, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("P-Court", StringComparison.Ordinal)).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WingsNarrowerThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrow()
    {
        // D-087 applies to every wing between two façades, the middle wings between two courts included: 9 m wings with the 4.57 m default depth
        // leave no core.
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(CourtClusterParameters.Default with { Depth = 9.0 });

        Result<IFloor> floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(plan);

        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, Assert.Single(floor.Diagnostics).Code);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingRebuildsEveryCourtsWallsOnEveryStorey()
    {
        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value;

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(building.Zones);
        Assert.All(zone.Parts, p => Assert.Equal(2, p.Footprint.Holes.Count));
        WallSurface[] court = building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnAnyCourt(w, 2)).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(5 * 2 * 4, court.Length);
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(2880.0, building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Ground).Sum(h => h.Area), 6);
    }

    /// <summary>Whether a wall lies on the edge of one of the courts of a plan with the default dimensions.</summary>
    private static bool OnAnyCourt(WallSurface wall, int courts) => Enumerable.Range(0, courts).Any(j =>
    {
        double west = 12.0 + (j * 36.0);
        return new[] { wall.Start, wall.End }.All(p => p.X >= west - 1e-9 && p.X <= west + 24.0 + 1e-9 && p.Y is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0);
    });

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static CourtClusterPresets Presets() => new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(CourtClusterParameters? parameters = null) =>
        new CourtClusterGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters ?? CourtClusterParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

## Slice B — `feature/operating-suite-court-cluster-components`

### Task 3: *Operating Suite* and *Court Cluster* components, with icons

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1 file, last changed in S8.5), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1 file, last changed in S8.5)
- Create (generated): `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/OperatingSuiteComponent.png`, `CourtClusterComponent.png`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `OperatingSuiteGenerator`, `CourtClusterGenerator`, and their records (Tasks 1 and 2); the S8.1 base `TypologyGeneratorComponent` (panel *2 Generate*, one *Plan* output, `AddPreset`, `TryNumbers`, `Preset`, `Output`, `FloorHeightDescription`, `OrientationDescription`); `ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter()` (S4.1); `IconLibrary.For`.
- Produces: two components after *Foyer Halls* in the file, each with its count as an integer input first, its number inputs defaulting to the ADR-006 defaults, *Floor Height*, *Orientation*, one required preset input per space type in the preset record's order (an unconnected one is Grasshopper's missing-input warning, D-065), and *Preview Location* last (D-062); the preset nicknames follow the nicknames of the preset components:

  | Component (nickname) | Inputs → output | Class | GUID |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | *Operating Suite* (OSuite) | Bank Count (BC, 2), Bank Length (BL, 36), Theatre Depth (TD, 7), Clean Corridor Width (CW, 3), Dirty Corridor Width (DW, 2.4), Support Length (SL, 12), Floor Height (FH, 4.2), Orientation (O, 0), Operating Theatre (OT), Clean Corridor (Clean), Dirty Corridor (Dirty), Clinical Support (CS), Preview Location (PL) → Plan | `OperatingSuiteComponent` | `33323afd-abf1-4ecc-bf54-5d67c411c962` |
  | *Court Cluster* (CCluster) | Court Count (CC, 2), Court Width (CW, 24), Court Depth (CD, 24), Depth (D, 12), Unit Width (UW, 7), Stair Width (SW, 3), Floor Height (FH, 3), Orientation (O, 0), Dwelling Unit (DU), Stair (St), Preview Location (PL) → Plan | `CourtClusterComponent` | `01fc2ca9-3ec0-4142-b827-2519a4523d36` |

  The motifs `operating_suite` (a support block in the west, dark dirty corridors at the top and bottom, a white clean corridor between two banks) and `court_cluster` (a block with two transparent court holes, a middle wing between them, dark stairs), in the Generate green, mapped after `FoyerHallsComponent`. 52 components, 6 parameters, and 59 icons. The fourteen earlier typology components and their base class are unchanged.

- [x] **Step 1: Create the branch**

```bash
git switch -c feature/operating-suite-court-cluster-components main
```

- [x] **Step 2: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (full file)**

Two usings and the two component classes at the end are added; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Lod.Generators.CourtCluster;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.OperatingSuite;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.RadialLobes;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output.
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type.</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.", GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class StairBayBarComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairBayBarComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Bay Bar",
            "SBar",
            "Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth, each stair serving the two dwellings beside it; no corridor. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairBayBarParameters d = StairBayBarParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Bay Count", "BC", "Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.BayCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of each stair along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stairs");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int bays = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref bays) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 5, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 6) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new StairBayBarParameters(bays, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4]);
        Output(DA, new StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new StairBayBarPresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Build, check Python, and see the icon script fail**

```bash
dotnet build BEMGen.sln -c Release
python -c "import sys, PIL; print(sys.version_info[:2] >= (3, 10), PIL.__version__)"
python scripts/make_icons.py
git status --short
```

Expected: `Build succeeded.`, `0 Warning(s)`, `0 Error(s)`; `True 12.3.0`; the script stops with exit code 1 and the message `Classes without an icon: ['CourtClusterComponent', 'OperatingSuiteComponent']; icons without a class: []`, and writes nothing (`git status --short` lists only ` M src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs`). With another Pillow version the script still runs, but the PNG bytes may differ from Step 5's hashes; then Step 6 decides.

- [x] **Step 4: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

The two motifs are added after `foyer_halls`, and their entries after *Foyer Halls* in the Generate group; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def office(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # An office block in elevation with a grid of windows.
    box(d, (16, 6, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (18, 38, 58):
        for x in (26, 52):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 16, y + 12), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((40, 76, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def core(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A service core: a box with two lift doors and an up and down arrow.
    box(d, (8, 8, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 34, 42, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((54, 34, 76, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.polygon([(48, 12), (38, 26), (58, 26)], fill=DARK)


def retail(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A shopping bag with its handle.
    d.arc((30, 6, 66, 46), start=180, end=360, fill=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.polygon([(14, 30), (82, 30), (90, 90), (6, 90)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A branching mall in plan: shop blocks (accent) along a T of public routes (white).
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 8, 56, 88), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((8, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE)
    for a, b in [((40, 4), (40, 40)), ((56, 4), (56, 92)), ((40, 56), (40, 92)), ((4, 40), (40, 40)), ((4, 56), (40, 56))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def kitchen(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cooking pot with a lid and two handles.
    box(d, (16, 40, 80, 86), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(4, 50), (16, 50)])
    line(d, [(80, 50), (92, 50)])
    d.rectangle((12, 28, 84, 38), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((42, 16, 54, 28), fill=DARK)


def dining(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A plate between a fork and a knife.
    d.ellipse((26, 22, 74, 70), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((38, 34, 62, 58), outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(12, 14), (12, 86)], width=6)
    line(d, [(84, 14), (84, 86)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((80, 14, 88, 46), fill=DARK)


def operating_theatre(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A medical cross.
    d.polygon(
        [(34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 34), (90, 34), (90, 62), (62, 62), (62, 90), (34, 90), (34, 62), (6, 62), (6, 34), (34, 34)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=LINE,
    )


def clean_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a white (clean) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((10, 42, 70, 54), fill=WHITE)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def dirty_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a hatched (dirty) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in range(12, 66, 14):
        line(d, [(x, 60), (x + 10, 36)], width=5)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def clinical_support(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Shelves of supplies beside a small medical cross.
    box(d, (6, 10, 58, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (34, 58):
        line(d, [(6, y), (58, y)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((70, 28, 82, 64), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((58, 40, 94, 52), fill=DARK)


def care_bedroom(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bed in elevation: headboard, mattress, and pillow.
    d.rectangle((6, 24, 16, 86), fill=DARK)
    box(d, (16, 54, 90, 72), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 42, 42, 54), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(86, 72), (86, 86)])


def care_communal(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A round table with four people around it.
    d.ellipse((28, 28, 68, 68), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for x, y in [(48, 12), (84, 48), (48, 84), (12, 48)]:
        d.ellipse((x - 9, y - 9, x + 9, y + 9), fill=DARK)


def lobby(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A glazed entrance front with an open door and an arrow leading in.
    box(d, (6, 10, 90, 88), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(34, 10), (34, 88)], width=4)
    line(d, [(62, 10), (62, 88)], width=4)
    d.rectangle((38, 44, 58, 88), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    arrow_head(d, (48, 84), "down", 8)


def activity_hall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A sports court in plan: the hall, its centre line, and the centre circle.
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(48, 16), (48, 80)], width=5)
    d.ellipse((34, 34, 62, 62), outline=DARK, width=5)


def service_rooms(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A row of three lockers with their handles.
    for left in (6, 36, 66):
        box(d, (left, 8, left + 24, 88), fill=accent, width=6)
        d.rectangle((left + 15, 40, left + 19, 56), fill=DARK)


def linear_generator(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Footprint with a west stair, a corridor band, and dwelling-unit divisions on both sides.
    box(d, (4, 12, 91, 83))
    d.rectangle((12, 20, 23, 75), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((28, 40, 87, 55), fill=accent)
    for x in (48, 68):
        line(d, [(x, 12), (x, 40)])
        line(d, [(x, 55), (x, 83)])


def stair_bay_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A long bar of two stair bays: dwelling (accent) | stair (dark) | dwelling, repeated, with a party wall between the bays.
    box(d, (2, 30, 93, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in (20, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 30, x + 10, 66), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(48, 30), (48, 66)], width=4)


def stair_pair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One compact plate: two dwellings (accent) around one full-depth stair (dark).
    box(d, (14, 22, 82, 74), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((42, 26, 54, 70), fill=DARK)


def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bar of dwellings (accent) between two end stairs (dark); the gallery along it is dashed, as it is not modelled.
    box(d, (4, 34, 91, 76), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((77, 38, 87, 72), fill=DARK)
    for x in (36, 54):
        line(d, [(x, 34), (x, 76)], width=6)
    for x in range(6, 90, 16):
        line(d, [(x, 20), (x + 9, 20)], width=6)


def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Attached houses in elevation: three gables sharing their side walls.
    houses = [(6, 34), (34, 62), (62, 90)]
    for left, right in houses:
        middle = (left + right) / 2
        d.polygon([(left, 46), (middle, 22), (right, 46), (right, 88), (left, 88)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def point_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A square plate: four dwellings (accent) in a pinwheel around one central core (dark) without façades.
    box(d, (6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((36, 36, 60, 60), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((6, 36), (60, 36)), ((60, 6), (60, 60)), ((36, 60), (90, 60)), ((36, 36), (36, 90))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def winged_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A comb in plan: a band (bottom) with three wings projecting north (up), every wing joined to the band along its root; stairs dark.
    d.polygon(
        [(4, 90), (92, 90), (92, 8), (70, 8), (70, 62), (59, 62), (59, 8), (37, 8), (37, 62), (26, 62), (26, 8), (4, 8)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    for left, right in ((4, 26), (37, 59), (70, 92)):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 30, right - 3, 38), fill=DARK)
    for x in (20, 48, 76):
        d.rectangle((x, 66, x + 6, 87), fill=DARK)


def open_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A U in plan: a north wing (top) across the full width and two side wings, around a court open to the south (bottom); stairs dark.
    d.polygon([(4, 92), (28, 92), (28, 34), (68, 34), (68, 92), (92, 92), (92, 6), (4, 6)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for x in (24, 45, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 9, x + 6, 31), fill=DARK)
    for left in (4, 68):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 58, left + 21, 66), fill=DARK)


def enclosed_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A closed ring in plan: south and north wings across the full width and west and east wings between them, around a court (the hole,
    # transparent) closed on all four sides; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 30, 66, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (7, 69):
        for x in (24, 66):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 6, y + 20), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 69):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 20, 51), fill=DARK)


def office_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangular plate: one office work area (accent) all around a central service core (dark).
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((32, 40, 64, 56), fill=DARK)


def cafeteria(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangle: the kitchen and servery along the north edge (dark) and the dining field (accent) with tables.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 14, 88, 34), fill=DARK)
    for y in (48, 68):
        for x in (20, 44, 68):
            d.ellipse((x, y, x + 10, y + 10), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.
    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).


def small_schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for x, h in ((28, 16), (40, 36), (52, 24), (64, 40)):
        d.rectangle((x, 72 - h, x + 7, 71), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 72, 75, 75), fill=DARK)


def small_load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(54, 20), (30, 52), (46, 52), (40, 76), (68, 44), (52, 44), (60, 20)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    d.ellipse((30, 42, 42, 54), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (20, 24, 75, 71), width=4)
    d.rectangle((24, 44, 71, 51), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 28, 31, 67), fill=DARK)
    for x in (48,):
        line(d, [(x, 24), (x, 44)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x, 52), (x, 71)], width=4)


def small_floor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (16, 36, 79, 59), fill=accent, width=4)


def small_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (32, 16, 63, 79), fill=accent, width=4)
    for y in (36, 56):
        line(d, [(32, y), (63, y)], width=4)


def radial_lobes(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cross in plan: four lobes (accent) on the sides of a square core (dark), each lobe split along its axis into two dwellings.
    d.polygon(
        [(32, 4), (64, 4), (64, 32), (92, 32), (92, 64), (64, 64), (64, 92), (32, 92), (32, 64), (4, 64), (4, 32), (32, 32)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    d.rectangle((32, 32, 64, 64), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((64, 48), (92, 48)), ((48, 4), (48, 32)), ((4, 48), (32, 48)), ((48, 64), (48, 92))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def branching_mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A T in plan: shop wings (accent) along a mall (dark) that branches from a central court into three branches, anchors at the branch ends.
    d.polygon([(4, 50), (34, 50), (34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 50), (92, 50), (92, 82), (4, 82)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.rectangle((18, 62, 78, 70), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((44, 20, 52, 70), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((18, 50), (18, 82)), ((78, 50), (78, 82)), ((34, 20), (62, 20))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def care_hub(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A communal hub (accent, a table in its middle) with three narrower bedroom wings (accent), each with a corridor (dark) along its axis.
    for xy in [(66, 49, 92, 75), (4, 49, 30, 75), (35, 6, 61, 42)]:
        box(d, xy, fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 42, 66, 82), fill=accent, width=6)
    for a, b in [((66, 62), (92, 62)), ((4, 62), (30, 62)), ((48, 6), (48, 42))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)
    d.ellipse((40, 54, 56, 70), fill=DARK)


def foyer_halls(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A foyer (white) with two large halls (accent) east and west, the support wing (dark) north, and its entrance from the south.
    box(d, (4, 40, 34, 74), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (62, 40, 92, 74), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (34, 18, 62, 40), fill=DARK, width=6)
    box(d, (34, 40, 62, 74), fill=WHITE, width=6)
    d.polygon([(48, 78), (40, 90), (56, 90)], fill=DARK)


def operating_suite(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Support rooms (accent) across the west end and bands to their east, south to north: a dirty corridor (dark), a theatre bank (accent), a
    # clean corridor (white), a theatre bank, and a dirty corridor.
    box(d, (4, 6, 92, 90), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(30, 6), (30, 90)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((30, 6, 92, 20), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((30, 76, 92, 90), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((30, 40, 92, 56), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((33, 44, 89, 52), fill=WHITE)


def court_cluster(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Wings around a row of two courts (the holes, transparent): south and north wings across the full width, and a west, middle, and east wing
    # between them; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent, width=6)
    for left in (18, 56):
        box(d, (left, 30, left + 22, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (19, 69):
        for x in (24, 46, 68):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 5, y + 8), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 43, 81):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 8, 51), fill=DARK)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint


ICONS: dict[str, tuple[Colour, Painter]] = {
    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),
    "BemGenInfoComponent": (INFO, component(info)),
    "ScheduleComponent": (PROGRAM, component(schedule)),
    "LoadComponent": (PROGRAM, component(load)),
    "ProgramPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(preset_tag)),
    "DwellingUnitPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(house)),
    "CorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(corridor)),
    "StairPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(stair)),
    "OfficePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(office)),
    "CorePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(core)),
    "RetailPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(retail)),
    "MallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(mall)),
    "KitchenPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(kitchen)),
    "DiningPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dining)),
    "OperatingTheatrePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(operating_theatre)),
    "CleanCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clean_corridor)),
    "DirtyCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dirty_corridor)),
    "ClinicalSupportPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clinical_support)),
    "CareBedroomPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_bedroom)),
    "CareCommunalPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_communal)),
    "LobbyPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(lobby)),
    "ActivityHallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(activity_hall)),
    "ServicePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(service_rooms)),
    "LinearPlanGeneratorComponent": (GENERATE, component(linear_generator)),
    "StairBayBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_bay_bar)),
    "StairPairComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_pair)),
    "GalleryBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(gallery_bar)),
    "TerraceRowComponent": (GENERATE, component(terrace_row)),
    "PointPlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(point_plate)),
    "WingedBandComponent": (GENERATE, component(winged_band)),
    "OpenCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(open_court)),
    "EnclosedCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(enclosed_court)),
    "OfficePlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(office_plate)),
    "CafeteriaComponent": (GENERATE, component(cafeteria)),
    "RadialLobesComponent": (GENERATE, component(radial_lobes)),
    "BranchingMallComponent": (GENERATE, component(branching_mall)),
    "CareHubComponent": (GENERATE, component(care_hub)),
    "FoyerHallsComponent": (GENERATE, component(foyer_halls)),
    "OperatingSuiteComponent": (GENERATE, component(operating_suite)),
    "CourtClusterComponent": (GENERATE, component(court_cluster)),
    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [x] **Step 6: Look at the icons**

Draw a contact sheet of all icons at 8× (nearest neighbour) into the build folder, which git ignores:

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

Expected: `59 icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png`.

Open the image. In the order of `sorted()` on Windows (case-insensitive), six per row: *Court Cluster* is the third icon of the third row, after *Corridor Preset* (a green block with two transparent court holes side by side, the middle wing between them, and dark stairs in the wings); *Operating Suite* the fifth icon of the sixth row, after *Open Court* (a green support block on the left and, to its right, dark dirty corridors at the top and bottom and a white clean corridor between two green banks). Every icon after each of them moves one place on, the 57 icons of S8.5 keep their motifs, and the 59 icons fill nine rows of six and five icons of a tenth.

If the hashes of Step 5 differed (another Pillow version), this check decides: when every icon matches the description above, continue and record the Pillow version in the commit message body; otherwise stop and report the difference. The script is never edited to match hashes.

2. Replace

```markdown

```

   with

```markdown
- Not modelled: individual theatres and their anaesthetic and preparation rooms (D-097), pressure cascades (only the presets' ventilation), lifts.
- Implemented (S8.6): as specified. Zones `SUP` (*Support*, 261.6 m²), then south to north `DC1` (*Dirty Corridor 1*, 86.4 m²), `TB1` (*Theatre Bank 1*, 252 m²), `CC1` (*Clean Corridor 1*, 108 m²), `TB2`, `DC2`; by default the plan is 48 m × 21.8 m (1046.4 m²). A corridor follows every bank, so *n* banks have *n* + 1 corridors, ⌈*n*/2⌉ clean and ⌊*n*/2⌋ + 1 dirty, and the northmost band is a dirty corridor for an even count and a clean one for an odd count (`CC2` with three banks). Every bank and corridor adjoins `SUP` over its full depth; every bank adjoins its two corridors over the bank length and no other bank; every corridor adjoins only banks and `SUP`. The outdoor walls are `SUP`'s west, south, and north sides, every band's east end, `DC1`'s south side, and the northmost corridor's north side, so a bank's only outdoor wall is its 7 m east end. The theatre preset's WWR is 0, so those walls get no window and no `WindowOmitted` info (ADR-011 rule 1: no glazed area, no window, no diagnostic); the default plan has 8 windows (71.988 m² of glazing): three on `SUP`, two on each dirty corridor, and one at the clean corridor's end. A theatre preset with glazing gives each bank one window on its east end. *Semantic Merge* keeps every zone, since no two zones of one type touch; *Perimeter Core* gives `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, and a `CORE` of 38.86 m × 12.66 m (491.9676 m²) over `SUP`, both banks, and `CC1`; `P-East` carries the glazing of every band's east end. The family has no upper bound; 2 to 5 banks are tested.
```

3. Replace

```markdown
- Not modelled: courts that are not in one row or not rectangular, the outdoor routes through the courts, a route hierarchy.
```

   with

```markdown
- Not modelled: courts that are not in one row or not rectangular, the outdoor routes through the courts, a route hierarchy.
- Implemented (S8.6): as specified, on `StairBayLayout` like `005`, which is unchanged: the two generators share the bay geometry and its fitting and differ only in where their wings and holes lie, so no further helper is extracted. With `k` courts the plan is `(k + 1)·D + k·w` wide; middle wing `i` (from 1) lies at `x ∈ [i·(D + w), i·(D + w) + D]` and the east wing at `x ∈ [k·(D + w), k·(D + w) + D]`, both over `y ∈ [D, D + c]`; court `j` (from 1) is the hole `[(j − 1)·(D + w) + D, j·(D + w)] × [D, D + c]`, stored clockwise, holes west to east. Zones `S-ST1…S-ST5`, `S-U1…S-U10` (*South Stair 1*, *South Unit 1*, …; five bays of 6.9 m dwellings in the 84 m south wing), the same for `N-…`, then `W-ST1`, `W-U1`, `W-U2`, `M1-ST1`, `M1-U1`, `M1-U2` (*Middle 1 Stair 1*, *Middle 1 Unit 1*, …), and `E-ST1`, `E-U1`, `E-U2` (one bay of 10.5 m dwellings in each 24 m wing, south to north); 2880 m² (84 m × 48 m less two 24 m × 24 m courts), 39 zones, 72 windows, 32 of them on the court façades, 16 per court (158.4 m² of the 374.4 m² of glazing). The zones of a middle wing have only court façades, west into one court and east into the next. Validation fits the stair bays to the south and north wings only for a valid `CourtCount`, since their length depends on it (`StairWidth 30 m leaves no dwelling width in each 84 m south and north wing: …`), and to the side wings (`… in each 24 m west, middle, and east wing: …`). *Semantic Merge* gives six dwelling zones, an L around each outer corner (374.4 m²) and a T where the middle wing meets the south or north wing (457.2 m²), and the thirteen stairs; no merged zone has a hole. *Perimeter Core* gives `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, one court zone per court and bin, `P-Court-North-1`, `P-Court-North-2`, … `P-Court-West-2` (130.5649 m² each, [ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)), and a `CORE` with one hole per court (712.5404 m²); 9 m wings fail it with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087). The family has no upper bound; up to 6 courts are tested in the generator's tests and 4 through every simplifier and aggregator.

#### Pipeline consequences of S8.6

Every plan simplifier and floor aggregator was run on both families, canonical, at the two rotated placements of the test suite, with the fewest and the most tested counts (2 and 5 banks, 2 and 4 courts), and with the most at 131.4°, and, while building the reference, in a sweep of 50 rotations from 0° to 353.5° with translations for the operating suite with 2, 3, and 5 banks, the court cluster with 2, 3, and 4 courts, one plan of each with other dimensions, and, as a regression, the enclosed court and the four S8.5 families with their fewest and most arms: 14 400 combinations without a failure. No step of `Lod.Core` changed, and no tolerance or check was relaxed:

- The court cluster is the first footprint with several holes. The surface builder and its boundary coverage, *No Simplification*, *Semantic Merge*, *Single Zone per Floor*, the four floor aggregators, and validation treat every hole as they treat the one hole of `005` ([ADR-002](ADR-002-geometry.md)).
- *Perimeter Core* offsets every hole by the bisector rule and unites the court trapezoids of one bin across all holes, as it did for one hole. The middle wings keep the bands of neighbouring courts apart, so each bin gives one polygon per court, numbered `P-Court-<Bin>-1`, `-2`, … in the union's output order, which is west to east on the canonical plan but is not a court number on a rotated plan. Its check that the offset holes lie apart from each other is what rejects middle wings not wider than twice the depth (`PlanTooNarrow`, D-087, [ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)).
- The theatre banks are the first generated zones whose outdoor walls get no window because their preset's WWR is 0; the base's window rule already places none and reports nothing for them (ADR-011), and every later step conserves the glazing that remains.
```

```markdown
- The court cluster (`SYN-TYP-011`, S8.6) has several holes; *Perimeter Core* gives each court its own court zone per bin, whose number follows the union's output order, not a court. ```markdown
```
```markdown
6. Trapezoids of the same bin are united, and every vertex of the union in the grid cell of a footprint vertex is replaced by that footprint vertex ([ADR-002](ADR-002-geometry.md)), so the perimeter zones keep the footprint's own vertices instead of vertices rounded to the clipping grid (S8.5); the mitred offset vertices keep their rounding. A bin that unites into one polygon becomes zone `P-<Bin>` ("Perimeter <Bin>"); a bin that yields several polygons becomes `P-<Bin>-1`, `P-<Bin>-2`, … in union output order, so every zone is one polygon. The trapezoids of the holes' edges, which face into the courts, are united per bin separately and become the court perimeter zones `P-Court-<Bin>` ("Perimeter Court <Bin>"), numbered in the same way; the bin is the direction the court façade faces, so the south wing's court façade belongs to `P-Court-North`. Zones are listed North, East, South, West, then the court zones North, East, South, West, then the core `CORE` ("Core"), which has a hole for each court.
```

A bin that unites into one polygon becomes zone `P-<Bin>` ("Perimeter <Bin>"); a bin that yields several polygons becomes `P-<Bin>-1`, `P-<Bin>-2`, … in union output order, so every zone is one polygon. The trapezoids of the holes' edges, which face into the courts, are united per bin separately and become the court perimeter zones `P-Court-<Bin>` ("Perimeter Court <Bin>"), numbered in the same way; with several holes the trapezoids of one bin are united across all holes, so each court gives its own polygon and `P-Court-<Bin>-1`, `-2`, … are numbered in union output order (S8.6); the bin is the direction the court façade faces, so the south wing's court façade belongs to `P-Court-North`. 3. Replace

```markdown
- Footprints with holes (S8.3, the enclosed court `HDHM-TYP-005` of [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)): every ring gets bisector trapezoids, so a court façade is zoned like an outer façade, and the core is a ring around the court. The court façades get their own zones because they are different façades from the outer façades of the same bin, facing a court instead of the street; uniting them with the outer zone of their bin would always give two separate polygons (the core lies between them), numbered `P-North-1`, `P-North-2` in an order that says nothing about which is which. For the default enclosed court (48 m × 48 m with a 24 m × 24 m court, 12 m wings) with `d` = 4.57 m: `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West` 198.4751 m² each, `P-Court-North` … `P-Court-West` 130.5649 m² each, and a `CORE` ring of 38.86² − 33.14² = 411.84 m². Every wing between the outer and a court façade must be wider than `2d` (D-087): 9 m wings fail with `PlanTooNarrow`. The bands of the outer and court façades never touch, so no trapezoid of a hole is ever united with one of the outer ring.
```

   with

```markdown
- Footprints with holes (S8.3, the enclosed court `HDHM-TYP-005` of [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)): every ring gets bisector trapezoids, so a court façade is zoned like an outer façade, and the core is a ring around the court. The court façades get their own zones because they are different façades from the outer façades of the same bin, facing a court instead of the street; uniting them with the outer zone of their bin would always give two separate polygons (the core lies between them), numbered `P-North-1`, `P-North-2` in an order that says nothing about which is which. For the default enclosed court (48 m × 48 m with a 24 m × 24 m court, 12 m wings) with `d` = 4.57 m: `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West` 198.4751 m² each, `P-Court-North` … `P-Court-West` 130.5649 m² each, and a `CORE` ring of 38.86² − 33.14² = 411.84 m². Every wing between the outer and a court façade must be wider than `2d` (D-087): 9 m wings fail with `PlanTooNarrow`. The bands of the outer and court façades never touch, so no trapezoid of a hole is ever united with one of the outer ring.
- Footprints with several holes (S8.6, the court cluster `SYN-TYP-011` of [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md), a row of courts between wings of stair bays) need no change. Every hole is offset by the same rule, and the court trapezoids of each bin are united across all holes; the bands of two courts never touch when the middle wing between them is wider than `2d`, so each bin gives one polygon per court, `P-Court-North-1`, `P-Court-North-2`, …, numbered in the union's output order. On the canonical plan that order is west to east; on a rotated plan the number is not tied to a court, as for the numbered outer zones. For the default court cluster (84 m × 48 m with two 24 m × 24 m courts, 12 m wings) with `d` = 4.57 m: `P-North` and `P-South` 362.9951 m², `P-East` and `P-West` 198.4751 m², eight court zones of 130.5649 m² (as for the enclosed court), and a `CORE` with two holes of 74.86 × 38.86 − 2 × 33.14² = 712.5404 m². A middle wing not wider than `2d` makes the offset holes of its two courts overlap, which the check that clipping the offset holes from the offset outer ring gives one polygon of the core's area rejects (`PlanTooNarrow`): 9 m wings fail. Naming the court zones by their court instead is left for a later ADR, if a study needs to tell the courts apart.
```

- [x] **Step 4: Edit `docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md`**

Make this replacement; the "Replace" text occurs exactly once in the file.

1. Replace

```markdown
**Footprints with holes (S8.3).** A court is a hole of the footprint, outside the building: its edges are outdoor walls with windows by the generator's rule, it has no zone, and no floor or ceiling covers it, so it adds neither ground nor roof area. Every plan simplifier and floor aggregator supports such footprints ([ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md) for *Perimeter Core*, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md) for the floor aggregators). In Grasshopper a polygon with holes is one planar face with an inner loop per hole, and a zone part one closed extrusion of that face. A zone may also have a hole inside a footprint without one: the office work area of `SYN-TYP-017` (S8.4, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)) is a ring around the core; the hole's edges are interzone walls with the zone inside it, and no step needed a change for it.
```

   with

```markdown
**Footprints with holes (S8.3).** A court is a hole of the footprint, outside the building: its edges are outdoor walls with windows by the generator's rule, it has no zone, and no floor or ceiling covers it, so it adds neither ground nor roof area. Every plan simplifier and floor aggregator supports such footprints ([ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md) for *Perimeter Core*, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md) for the floor aggregators). In Grasshopper a polygon with holes is one planar face with an inner loop per hole, and a zone part one closed extrusion of that face. A zone may also have a hole inside a footprint without one: the office work area of `SYN-TYP-017` (S8.4, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)) is a ring around the core; the hole's edges are interzone walls with the zone inside it, and no step needed a change for it. A footprint may have several holes: the court cluster of `SYN-TYP-011` (S8.6) has one per court, and no step needed a change for them either.
```

6. Replace

```markdown
- *Semantic Merge* merges connected zones of the same space type; each group's footprint is the union of its zones as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`), which keeps the zones' own vertices and may have holes: the four dwellings of the point plate become one ring around the core (ADR-002). *Perimeter Core* makes one perimeter zone per orientation plus a core, with corners split on the bisectors (ADR-008), also at re-entrant corners; an orientation with several separate façade runs, such as the open court's three south façades, gives the zones `P-South-1…`. A footprint with holes is offset on every ring: the court façades get their own zones `P-Court-North`, `P-Court-East`, `P-Court-South`, `P-Court-West`, named by the direction they face and listed after the outer zones, and the core is a ring around the court (ADR-008, S8.3). The perimeter zones keep the footprint's own vertices, not vertices rounded to the clipping grid, so a façade edge shorter than about 1 m, such as a hub corner of the care hub, is covered by the single-zone aggregators on a rotated plan (ADR-008, S8.5). Every wing or other part of the footprint, including the wings between the outer and the court façades, must be wider than twice the depth, or the simplifier fails with `PlanTooNarrow`, whose message says so. *Single Zone per Floor* makes the floor one zone.
```

   with

```markdown
- *Semantic Merge* merges connected zones of the same space type; each group's footprint is the union of its zones as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`), which keeps the zones' own vertices and may have holes: the four dwellings of the point plate become one ring around the core (ADR-002). *Perimeter Core* makes one perimeter zone per orientation plus a core, with corners split on the bisectors (ADR-008), also at re-entrant corners; an orientation with several separate façade runs, such as the open court's three south façades, gives the zones `P-South-1…`. A footprint with holes is offset on every ring: the court façades get their own zones `P-Court-North`, `P-Court-East`, `P-Court-South`, `P-Court-West`, named by the direction they face and listed after the outer zones, and the core is a ring around the court (ADR-008, S8.3). With several courts the court trapezoids of each bin are united across the courts, giving one zone per court and bin, `P-Court-North-1`, `P-Court-North-2`, …, numbered in the union's output order, and a core with one hole per court (ADR-008, S8.6). The perimeter zones keep the footprint's own vertices, not vertices rounded to the clipping grid, so a façade edge shorter than about 1 m, such as a hub corner of the care hub, is covered by the single-zone aggregators on a rotated plan (ADR-008, S8.5). Every wing or other part of the footprint, including the wings between the outer and the court façades and between two courts, must be wider than twice the depth, or the simplifier fails with `PlanTooNarrow`, whose message says so. *Single Zone per Floor* makes the floor one zone.
```

```markdown
- **Preview Location (D-062).** The fifteen plan generators, *Transform Plan*, the four plan simplifiers, and the four floor aggregators have an optional last input *Preview Location* (PL): a vector in model units, default `0,0,0` (a point converts to a vector). It moves only that component's viewport preview: the plan, floor, or building data, *Inspect*, *Validate*, and *Convert2BEM* keep the true coordinates, and downstream components do not inherit the move (each wraps its output with its own input). *Convert2BEM* has no such input.
```

1. Replace

```markdown
S4.1 adds one default preset component per space type of the linear plan, *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset*, and deletes *Example Residential Presets* (D-061). Every input of a default preset component has a default, the values of §2, so the component works with nothing connected: Name, WWR, Conditioned, Heating (°C), Cooling (°C), and per load a value input (people/m², W/m², or 1/h, as in its description) and an optional schedule input that replaces the built-in schedule when connected.
```
