```markdown
### S8.6 checklist — operating suite and court cluster (`v0.8.5`)

S8.6 adds two plan generator components in *2 Generate* ([ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)): the operating suite with separated clean and dirty routes (`SYN-TYP-015`) and the court cluster around several courts (`SYN-TYP-011`), the first footprint with several holes ([ADR-008](../decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)). No existing component, input, output, or GUID changes.

#### Toolbar and icons

- [ ] *2 Generate* shows, after *Foyer Halls*, *Operating Suite* (a support block on the left and bands to its right: dark dirty corridors at the top and bottom, a white clean corridor between two green banks) and *Court Cluster* (a block with two court holes and a middle wing between them), each with its own green icon.
- [ ] *Operating Suite* (OSuite) inputs, in order, with their defaults: Bank Count 2, Bank Length 36, Theatre Depth 7, Clean Corridor Width 3, Dirty Corridor Width 2.4, Support Length 12, Floor Height 4.2, Orientation 0, Operating Theatre, Clean Corridor, Dirty Corridor, Clinical Support, Preview Location; one output, *Plan*.
- [ ] *Court Cluster* (CCluster): Court Count 2, Court Width 24, Court Depth 24, Depth 12, Unit Width 7, Stair Width 3, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location.
- [ ] The count inputs are integers. With a preset input unconnected, each generator shows Grasshopper's missing-input warning and outputs nothing (D-065); a *Clean Corridor Preset* on the *Dirty Corridor* input gives `Error PresetSpaceTypeMismatch [DirtyCorridor]: Input DirtyCorridor needs a DirtyCorridor preset, got 'Example Clean Corridor' (CleanCorridor).`

#### Default plans

1. Place *Operating Suite* with *Operating Theatre Preset*, *Clean Corridor Preset*, *Dirty Corridor Preset*, and *Clinical Support Preset* connected; connect *Inspect* to its *Plan*.
   - [ ] No runtime message; a 48 m × 21.8 m plan of 1046.4 m², 4.2 m high: `SUP` at x = 0–12 m over the full depth, then from south to north `DC1` (y = 0–2.4 m), `TB1`, `CC1`, `TB2`, `DC2` (y = 19.4–21.8 m), each at x = 12–48 m.
   - [ ] 8 windows, 71.988 m² of glazing: three on `SUP`, two on each dirty corridor, one at the clean corridor's east end; the banks' east walls have no window (WWR 0) and no `WindowOmitted` remark.
   - [ ] The *Inspect* report equals `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OperatingSuite/Snapshots/operating-suite-canonical.txt`.
2. Place *Court Cluster* with *Dwelling Unit Preset* and *Stair Preset* connected; connect *Inspect*.
   - [ ] No runtime message; an 84 m × 48 m block of 2880 m² with two courts as holes at x = 12–36 m and x = 48–72 m, y = 12–36 m; 39 zones: `S-…` and `N-…` (five stairs and ten dwellings each), `W-…`, `M1-…`, `E-…` (one stair and two dwellings each).
   - [ ] 72 windows, 374.4 m² of glazing, 32 of them (158.4 m²) on the court façades, facing into the courts; `M1-U1` has windows to the west and the east.
   - [ ] The *Inspect* report equals `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/CourtCluster/Snapshots/court-cluster-canonical.txt`.

#### Combinations

3. Connect each plan to the four plan simplifiers (*Perimeter Core* Depth 4.57) and each floor three times, Multipliers 1, 3, 1, to the four floor aggregators.
   - [ ] Operating suite: *Semantic Merge* keeps all six zones; *Perimeter Core* gives `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, and a `CORE` of 491.9676 m².
   - [ ] Court cluster: *Semantic Merge* gives six dwelling zones and thirteen stairs, none with a hole; *Perimeter Core* gives `P-North` to `P-West`, `P-Court-North-1`, `P-Court-North-2` to `P-Court-West-2` (130.5649 m² each), and a `CORE` of 712.5404 m² with two holes; *Single Zone Building* previews one solid with two courts through every storey.
   - [ ] For all 16 combinations of each plan, *Validate* gives Passed = True (only `ConditionedFloorArea` may read `note`), no aggregator shows a warning, and *Convert2BEM* with Override = False converts without an error; every zone Brep has a positive volume (D-091).

#### Counts and rotated placement

4. Set Bank Count to 3 and 5 and Court Count to 3 and 4, and repeat step 3 for each.
   - [ ] Bank Count 3: the northmost band is `CC2`, a clean corridor with a north façade; Court Count 3: two middle wings `M1-…` and `M2-…` and three holes; *Perimeter Core* gives `P-Court-<Bin>-1` to `-3`.
   - [ ] *Validate* gives Passed = True for every combination.
5. Put each default plan through *Transform Plan* with a 30° rotation and a move by 5.3, −2.1, 0, then through each simplifier and aggregator (Multipliers 1, 3, 1); repeat with 131.4° and 15.08, −153.9, 0, and with Bank Count 5 and Court Count 4 at 131.4°.
   - [ ] *Validate* gives Passed = True for all 16 combinations of each placement, with the same total glazing as the unrotated plan.

#### Parameters

6. Change inputs one at a time and set each back afterwards.
   - [ ] *Operating Suite* Bank Count 1: `Error InvalidParameter: BankCount must be at least 2 (the theatre banks repeat beside the support rooms), got 1.`; Bank Length 0: `Error InvalidParameter: BankLength must be positive, got 0.`
   - [ ] *Operating Theatre Preset* WWR 0.2: the operating suite has 10 windows, one on the east end of each bank.
   - [ ] *Court Cluster* Court Count 1: `Error InvalidParameter: CourtCount must be at least 2 (one court is SYN-TYP-005, Enclosed Court), got 1.`
   - [ ] *Court Cluster* Unit Width 0.5 and Stair Width 30: `Error InvalidParameter: StairWidth 30 m leaves no dwelling width in each 84 m south and north wing: its 3 stair bays need 90 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.` and `… in each 24 m west, middle, and east wing: its one stair bay needs 30 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.`
   - [ ] *Court Cluster* Depth 9 through *Perimeter Core* (Depth 4.57): `Error PlanTooNarrow: Perimeter depth 4.57 m leaves no valid core for this footprint: every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.` and no floor; the other three simplifiers still work.
```

- [x] **Step 10: Check the code fences of the edited documents**

Check the code fences of the seven documents of this task: every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, and no file ends inside a block.

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path

fence = "`" * 3
names = [
    "docs/architecture/pipeline.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md",
    "docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md",
    "docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md",
    "docs/research/program-presets.md",
]
for name in names:
    lines = Path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
    inside, blocks, problems = False, 0, []
    for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        mark = line.strip()
        if not mark.startswith(fence):
            continue
        if not inside and mark == fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block without a language")
        if inside and mark != fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block inside a block")
        blocks += inside
        inside = not inside
    if inside:
        problems.append("the file ends inside a block")
    print(f"{name}: {blocks} code blocks, " + ("; ".join(problems) or "fences balanced"))
EOF
```

docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md: 2 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced

`SYN-TYP-001` to `009` are the *High-Density Housing* families `HDHM-TYP-001` to `009`, D-099: `LinearPlanGenerator`, the double-sided internal corridor bar `SYN-TYP-009`; the bar families `StairBayBarGenerator` 001, `StairPairGenerator` 008, `GalleryBarGenerator` 002 with its gallery not modelled, and `TerraceRowGenerator` 006 with one dwelling zone per plot and storey, D-085; the non-rectangular families `PointPlateGenerator` 007, a square plate whose core has no outdoor wall, `WingedBandGenerator` 003, a comb, and `OpenCourtGenerator` 004, a U; `EnclosedCourtGenerator` 005, four wings around a court closed on all four sides, the first footprint with a hole; and the non-residential `OfficePlateGenerator` `SYN-TYP-017`, whose office work area is one ring-shaped zone around a central `Core` zone, `CafeteriaGenerator` `SYN-TYP-016`, a `Kitchen` zone along the whole north edge of a larger `Dining` zone; the hub-and-arms families on the shared `HubArmsLayout`, a square hub with up to four arms (D-100, D-102): `RadialLobesGenerator` 010, `BranchingMallGenerator` 014, `CareHubGenerator` 018, and `FoyerHallsGenerator` 019; and the operating suite `OperatingSuiteGenerator` 015, theatre banks between alternating clean and dirty corridors beside the support rooms, and the court cluster `CourtClusterGenerator` 011, stair-bay wings around a row of courts, the first footprint with several holes (D-104); the non-residential ones zoned one zone per department, D-097; one typed preset per space type, D-061; optionally rotated and moved by `PlanTransform`, D-070) → plan simplifiers (`NoSimplification`, `SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`) → floor aggregators (`Stack`, `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`, D-071; floors and ceilings designated on the assembled stack, D-068) → validation → `Convert2BEM` (heat transfer between zones an export option, off by default, D-069), as described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md). Every generator places one centred window per outdoor wall (D-026), except on a wall shorter than 1 m or where the window would be narrower or lower than 0.3 m (`GeometryLimits`, info `WindowOmitted`, [ADR-011](docs/decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md)). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt (`SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`), every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the source windows it covers (`CentredWindows`, D-079, [ADR-012](docs/decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)); the other steps keep their input's windows. Merged zones are unions of their sources as tiles that keep the sources' own vertices, so they may have holes and stay exact on rotated plans, and vertices on edges are recognised at their original positions (D-088, [ADR-002](docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md)). Every step supports footprints with holes: a court is outside the building, its edges are outdoor walls facing into it, every footprint edge, court edges included, must be covered by outdoor zone edges (ADR-002), and `PerimeterCore` gives the court façades their own zones `P-Court-<Bin>` around a core that is a ring, several of them in the court cluster, where *Perimeter Core* gives each court its own `P-Court-<Bin>-k` zones ([ADR-008](docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)); `PerimeterCore` needs every part of a footprint, including the wings between the outer and the court façades, to be wider than twice its depth and otherwise fails with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087). Its perimeter zones keep the footprint's own vertices, so short façades stay covered on rotated plans (ADR-008, S8.5). Vertical aggregation follows [ADR-013](docs/decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md), which supersedes [ADR-009](docs/decisions/ADR-009-vertical-aggregation.md) where it differs. In *Convert2BEM* and the previews, zone solids are outward-oriented and every surface and window faces out of its zone (D-091). ADR-006, ADR-011, ADR-012, ADR-013, ADR-014, and the S4.2, S5, S8.1, S8.2, S8.3, S8.4, S8.5, and S8.6 plans were accepted by the controller and await the owner's reading (D-075, D-080, D-084, D-098). In Grasshopper every component has an icon drawn by `scripts/make_icons.py` (D-063), and the generator, *Transform Plan*, simplifier, and aggregator components have a *Preview Location* input that moves only their preview (D-062). Until the owner says otherwise, changes need not keep backward compatibility, but component GUIDs never change (D-064).

The precedent set is the four-source family set `SYN-TYP-001` to `SYN-TYP-019` of every program type, housing and non-residential ([docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md](docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md), D-093, D-094); `SYN-TYP-013` is set aside (D-096), non-residential floors are zoned one zone per department (D-097, [ADR-014](docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)), and BEMGen documents use the `SYN-TYP-*` IDs (D-099). The families `017` and `016` were built in S8.4, `010`, `014`, `018`, `019` in S8.5, the hub-and-arms families with at most four arms because the shared layout is axis-aligned (D-100), and `015` and `011` in S8.6; `012` (S8.7) is specified in ADR-006 and built before S6 (D-098).

Not implemented yet: the stepped bands of S8.7 (`SYN-TYP-012`), `Lod.Export` and Convert2IDF (S6), and Grasshopper UX and release (S9). The precedent study of S7 was carried out in a separate repository (D-082); its nine families are paraphrased in [docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md](docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md). A zone only about 1 m wide on a rotated plan can fail validation after a merging simplifier, a known limit of clipping on the grid (D-090). The example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values, and the `Convert2BEM` output layout stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023).
```

```markdown
Checkpoint 1 reached: stages S0–S4 of the [implementation roadmap](docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) are complete (tag `v0.4.0`), followed by the revision stages S4.1 (tag `v0.4.1`) and S4.2 (tag `v0.4.2`), stage S5 (tag `v0.5.0`), and sub-stages S8.1 (tag `v0.8.0`), S8.2 (tag `v0.8.1`), S8.3 (tag `v0.8.2`), S8.4 (tag `v0.8.3`), S8.5 (tag `v0.8.4`), and S8.6 (tag `v0.8.5`). In Rhino 8 the plugin runs the whole pipeline for seventeen plan generators: program presets with conditioning (a default preset component per space type, housing and non-residential); the linear plan generator and the eight other typologies of the *High-Density Housing* precedent study (`SYN-TYP-001`–`009`, ADR-006), the bars *Stair Bay Bar*, *Stair Pair*, *Gallery Bar*, and *Terrace Row*, the non-rectangular *Point Plate*, *Winged Band*, and *Open Court*, *Enclosed Court*, whose footprint has the court as a hole, the non-residential *Office Plate* (`SYN-TYP-017`, a work-area ring around a central core) and *Cafeteria* (`SYN-TYP-016`, a kitchen and servery edge beside a dining field), and the branching plans on one hub-and-arms layout, *Radial Lobes* (`SYN-TYP-010`), *Branching Mall* (`SYN-TYP-014`), *Care Hub* (`SYN-TYP-018`), *Foyer Halls* (`SYN-TYP-019`), the operating suite *Operating Suite* (`SYN-TYP-015`, theatre banks between separate clean and dirty corridors), and *Court Cluster* (`SYN-TYP-011`, wings around a row of courts, one hole per court), the non-residential ones zoned one zone per department (D-097, ADR-014), each with one preset input per space type and explicit windows (none on a wall shorter than 1 m or for a window below 0.3 m, ADR-011); *Transform Plan* to rotate and move a plan; four plan simplifiers (Z0–Z3); four floor aggregators (Stack, StackedFloorZoneMultiplier, SingleZonePerFloorType, SingleZoneBuilding) that designate floors and ceilings on the assembled stack and warn about unsupported storeys; invariant validation; and `Convert2BEM`, which writes heat transfer between zones only when asked (off by default). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt, every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the windows it covers, so glazing is conserved per wall, façade, orientation, and building (D-079); merged zones stay exact on rotated plans (D-088); every simplifier and aggregator supports footprints with one or more holes, with the court façades as outdoor walls; and zone solids, surfaces, and windows are oriented out of their zones (D-091). Every component has an icon, and every component with a viewport preview has a *Preview Location* input that moves only its preview. The pipeline is described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md); the example definition `examples/grasshopper/checkpoint-1-pipeline.gh`, which wires every simplifier into every aggregator, is still to be built by a person. Next: S8.7 (the stepped bands of `SYN-TYP-012`, with plans per storey, D-095, D-098), then Convert2IDF (S6) and Grasshopper UX and release (S9). The `Convert2BEM` output stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023), and the example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values.
```

from pathlib import Path

fence = "`" * 3
path = Path("docs/decisions/decision-log.md")
lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
stray = [number for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1) if line == fence]
assert stray == [705, 715], stray
assert lines[703].startswith("- `Lod.Generators.csproj` makes its internals visible") and lines[705] == "", lines[703:706]

for number in reversed(stray):
    del lines[number - 1]
path.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
print("removed the stray fence lines", " and ".join(map(str, stray)), "of", path.as_posix())
EOF

```markdown
### D-104 — The layout choices of the operating suite and the court cluster

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-098) · **Status:** Provisional
- Why: [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md) fixes the records, defaults, zone IDs, and band and wing order of `SYN-TYP-015` and `SYN-TYP-011`, but not the corridor count that follows from the band order, how *Perimeter Core* zones several courts, or whether the court cluster shares more code with the enclosed court; the S8.6 reference settled these.
- Operating suite: a corridor follows every bank and the corridors alternate from a dirty one in the south, so *n* banks have ⌈n/2⌉ clean and ⌊n/2⌋ + 1 dirty corridors, numbered separately (`DC1, TB1, CC1, TB2, DC2, TB3, CC2, …`); with an odd count the northmost band is a clean corridor. The theatre banks' only outdoor walls are their east ends, which the example theatre preset's WWR 0 leaves without a window and without `WindowOmitted` (ADR-011 rule 1). The generator tests' window check (`PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall`) now requires no window on an outdoor wall whose zone's preset has WWR 0, a stricter rule that every earlier generator test still passes.
- Court cluster: shares `StairBayLayout` with the enclosed court and nothing else, so `EnclosedCourtGenerator` and its snapshot are unchanged; middle wings are named *Middle i*; the stair bays of the south and north wings are checked only for a valid `CourtCount`.
- *Perimeter Core* with several holes is unchanged: the court trapezoids of one bin are united across all holes, so each court gives its own zone per bin, numbered `P-Court-<Bin>-1…` in the union's output order, which is west to east on the canonical plan but is not a court number on a rotated plan ([ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)). Naming court zones per court is left to a later ADR.
```

```markdown
### D-105 — S8.6 complete

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-098) · **Status:** Provisional
- Sub-stage S8.6 (D-098) is complete and tagged `v0.8.5`; after its gate and a fresh clone of the tag pass `scripts/verify.ps1`, `main` and `v0.8.5` are pushed (D-098). The two families are implemented as [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md) specifies them, with no record, default, or rule changed (D-104). New generators and components in *2 Generate*, with icons: *Operating Suite* (`OperatingSuiteGenerator`, `SYN-TYP-015`, OSuite, `33323afd-abf1-4ecc-bf54-5d67c411c962`): a `ClinicalSupport` zone across the west end and bands of `OperatingTheatre` banks between alternating `DirtyCorridor` and `CleanCorridor` zones, every bank between one clean and one dirty corridor, no clean corridor beside a dirty one, every band adjoining the support rooms; *Court Cluster* (`CourtClusterGenerator`, `SYN-TYP-011`, CCluster, `01fc2ca9-3ec0-4142-b827-2519a4523d36`): wings of stair bays around a row of at least two courts, each middle wing bordering two courts, the first footprint with several holes.
- No step of `Lod.Core` changed: every simplifier, aggregator, and validation handles several holes, and *Perimeter Core* gives each court its own court zones per orientation ([ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md), [ADR-002](ADR-002-geometry.md)); wings not wider than twice the perimeter depth, between two courts included, fail it with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087). No tolerance or check was relaxed.
- The twenty-two existing snapshots are byte-identical; two canonical snapshots are new. 193 core, 427 generator, and 1385 integration tests pass on `net8.0`, including both families with every simplifier and aggregator on the canonical plan, rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m and rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m, with the fewest and the most tested counts (2 and 5 banks, 2 and 4 courts), and with the most at 131.4°; a sweep of 50 rotations for both families and, as a regression, the enclosed court and the S8.5 families found no failure in 14 400 combinations.

It is recorded in `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` and the close-out commit; the viewport look, the icons in the toolbar, and example definitions stay open for a person (D-058).

- S8.7 (`SYN-TYP-012`, plans per storey, D-095) can start.

- [x] **Step 10: Check the code fences of every document of the stage**

Check the code fences of every Markdown file this stage touches, this plan included, and the entries and sections appended to the decision log and the smoke-test guide: every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, and no file ends inside a block.

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
import re
from pathlib import Path

fence = "`" * 3
names = [
    "AGENTS.md",
    "README.md",
    "docs/architecture/pipeline.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md",
    "docs/decisions/decision-log.md",
    "docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md",
    "docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md",
    "docs/plans/2026-10-02-s8.6-operating-suite-court-cluster.md",
    "docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md",
    "docs/research/program-presets.md",
]
for name in names:
    lines = Path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
    inside, blocks, problems = False, 0, []
    for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        mark = line.strip()
        if not mark.startswith(fence):
            continue
        if not inside and mark == fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block without a language")
        if inside and mark != fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block inside a block")
        blocks += inside
        inside = not inside
    if inside:
        problems.append("the file ends inside a block")
    print(f"{name}: {blocks} code blocks, " + ("; ".join(problems) or "fences balanced"))
log = Path("docs/decisions/decision-log.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
guide = Path("docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
entries = [part for part in log.split("\n### ")[1:] if part.startswith("D-10")]
sections = [part for part in re.split(r"\n(?=#{3,4} )", guide) if re.match(r"#{3,4} .*S8\.[56]", part)]
for kind, parts in (("decision-log entries D-100 to D-105", entries), ("smoke-test sections of S8.5 and S8.6", sections)):
    ending = [part.split("\n")[0] for part in parts if part.rstrip().split("\n")[-1].strip().startswith(fence)]
    print(f"{len(parts)} {kind}, ending with a fence line: " + (", ".join(ending) or "none"))
EOF
```

### Task 7: Fresh-clone gate and push (controller, D-098)

**Files:** none

D-098 pushes each sub-stage after its gate: the controller performs this task itself (a push is never delegated), and never with force. Its steps stay unticked in the plan, because they run after the close-out commit.

- [ ] **Step 1: Check the state to push**

```bash

git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/main main && echo fast-forward
git tag --list "v0.8.*"
git ls-remote --tags origin "v0.8.*"
```

Expected: no output from `git status --short`; the two SHAs are equal (the tag points at the tip of `main`); `fast-forward` (local `main` contains `origin/main`, which was at `16ead1f` when this plan was written); the tags `v0.8.0` to `v0.8.5`; `ls-remote` shows which of them the remote has. If `origin/main` has commits that local `main` does not, or the remote already has a `v0.8.5` that points at another commit, stop and report: nothing is pushed, and no rebase or force push is attempted. If the remote lacks one of `v0.8.0` to `v0.8.4`, report it to the person; this task pushes only `main` and `v0.8.5`.

```

Expected: the clone reports a detached `HEAD` at `v0.8.5`; verify builds the clone with `0 Warning(s)` and `0 Error(s)` and ends with `VERIFY PASSED`, with 193 core, 427 generators, and 1385 integration tests passed on net8.0; `describe` prints `v0.8.5`. Then delete the clone (`rm -rf ../BEMGen-v0.8.5-clone` in Git Bash). If verify fails, stop: nothing is pushed.

- [ ] **Step 3: Push (no force)**

```bash

git push origin v0.8.5
git ls-remote --tags origin "v0.8.5*"
git status -sb
```

Expected: each push succeeds without `--force` (`main` as a fast-forward, the tag as `[new tag]`); `ls-remote` lists `v0.8.5` with its peeled `^{}` line; `git status -sb` shows `## main...origin/main` with nothing ahead or behind. A rejected push is not retried with force: stop and report.

## Exit criteria

- `OperatingSuiteGenerator` (015) and `CourtClusterGenerator` (011) implement ADR-006 with their defaults, zone IDs and order, and validation (at least 2 banks, at least 2 courts, no upper bound); each has tiling, adjacency, window, count, invalid-parameter, preset, determinism, provenance, and snapshot tests that failed to build on the code before them; the court cluster's footprint has one hole per court and every middle wing borders two courts.
- The generator tests' window check expects no window on a wall whose zone's preset has WWR 0, and every earlier generator test passes it.
- Both families validate with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on their canonical plans, at two rotated and moved placements, with their fewest and most tested counts, and with the most at 131.4° (99 and 102 cases); *Perimeter Core* gives every court its own court zones per orientation, and 9 m wings fail it with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087); no `Lod.Core` file changed.
- Two generator components in *2 Generate*, with the GUIDs of this plan, their defaults, integer count inputs, required preset inputs, *Preview Location* last, and icons (59 icons, the existing ones byte-identical); no existing component, input, or GUID changed.
- Snapshots: the twenty-two existing snapshots byte-identical; two new canonical snapshots.
- ADR-006, ADR-008, ADR-002, `pipeline.md`, `syn-typologies.md`, and `program-presets.md` describe S8.6; the S8.6 smoke-test checklist and its headless result are in the guide.
- Every Markdown file the stage touches has balanced code fences, with no stray fence line left by S8.5.
- `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 193 core, 427 generators, 1385 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- Version 0.8.5, tag `v0.8.5`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line and detailed-plans row, and the decision-log entries D-104 and D-105 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done.
- After a fresh clone of `v0.8.5` passes `scripts/verify.ps1`, `main` and `v0.8.5` are pushed without force (D-098).

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block, snapshot, and documentation replacement of Tasks 1 to 4 was extracted programmatically from a verified reference implementation built on `main` at `16ead1f`, 4 commits from `a3dda20` to `373af00`, each passing `scripts/verify.ps1` with 0 warnings; the tasks keep the reference's commit order, one task per commit (no reordering was needed: a relative-link check of every reference commit found no link to a file that a later commit creates). The only content not taken from the reference is this plan's own commit, the two fence repairs and the fence checks (Task 4, Steps 8 and 10; Task 6, Steps 8 and 10), and the close-out of Task 6 (adapted from the reference's proposed texts). Every task was then replayed on a worktree of `main` at `16ead1f` (its branch standing in for `main`; nothing fetched, pulled, tagged, or pushed), taking each file content, each replacement, and each script from this plan's text: the stricter window check against every earlier generator test, the red build errors of Tasks 1 and 2 (with line and column numbers and in the compiler's order), the single failing snapshot test of Tasks 1 and 2 and the received snapshots (byte-identical to the expected content), the failure of `make_icons.py` before Task 3 and the 59 hashes after it (every PNG byte-identical to the reference's), the counts after every task, a 0-warning build after every task, every documentation replacement (each "Replace" text occurs exactly once when it is applied), the fence repairs and checks, the link checks, the close-out edits, and `VERIFY PASSED` on every merge are observed results. The replay's end state equals the reference: `git diff --stat ref/s8.6 replay/s8.6` limited to the 21 paths the reference changes prints nothing for the state before the close-out, and after it lists only `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md`, whose difference is the result section of Task 6, Step 7; `Directory.Build.props` (0.8.5), AGENTS.md, README.md, the decision log, the roadmap, and this plan are files the reference does not change. Every observation of a first replay was observed again, unchanged, in a second replay of the final plan from a fresh worktree.
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap names no slices for S8.6; this plan uses three slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `feature/operating-suite-court-cluster` (Tasks 1–2), `feature/operating-suite-court-cluster-components` (Task 3), `docs/architecture-operating-suite-court-cluster` (Tasks 4–5), and `chore/repo-s8.6-close-out`. Merges rebase onto local `main`. As in S8.4 and S8.5, the stage is pushed at its end (D-098): Task 7 is the controller's, after the tag and a fresh clone of it pass `scripts/verify.ps1`. `origin/main` was at `16ead1f` when this plan was written, so the push is a fast-forward.
3. **Links.** A relative-link check (file targets and heading anchors, GitHub's slug rule) over every Markdown file of the replay found: no broken link at the plan commit or after any commit of the replay (checked after Tasks 2, 4, 5, and 6). Task 4 renames the heading `## Grasshopper components through S8.5` of `pipeline.md` to `… through S8.6` and updates its two links (in `pipeline.md` and `program-presets.md`) in the same commit.
4. **The baseline is 193 core, 360 generators, and 1184 integration tests** (D-103).
5. **Tests that were not test-first, and why.** The integration matrices of Tasks 1 and 2 (201 cases) passed at once in the reference and in the replay: the surface builder, the four simplifiers, the four aggregators, and validation already handle bands of rectangles and footprints with several holes; each task says to stop if a case fails. *Perimeter Core* with several holes needed no change, so it has no red phase; the integration tests of Task 2 check its result (court zones per court and bin, as many core holes as courts, the court glazing conserved) and its precondition on 9 m wings. Task 3 is Grasshopper code, checked by the build, the embedded icons, and Task 5. The build-error red phases list only declaration-level errors: the compiler stops before binding method bodies. The stricter window check of Task 1 changes a test helper, not a test; Step 3 runs it against every earlier generator test before the new tests exist.
6. **Window rule with WWR 0 (D-104).** `PlaceWindowsByRatio` computes a glazed area of 0 for a zone whose preset has WWR 0, skips the length check (it requires a positive glazed area), and `Window.Centered` returns no window, so the theatre banks' east ends get no window and no `WindowOmitted` (ADR-011, rule 1). Before Task 1 the helper `HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall` would have required one window on every outdoor wall; it now requires none on such a wall. A glazed theatre preset (WWR 0.2) gives 10 windows, tested.
7. **Choices of the reference (D-104).** The corridor count follows from ADR-006's band order: a corridor after every bank, alternating from a dirty one, so *n* banks have ⌈*n*/2⌉ clean and ⌊*n*/2⌋ + 1 dirty corridors and the northmost band is a clean corridor for an odd count (`CC2` with three banks). `EnclosedCourtGenerator` is untouched: both generators use `StairBayLayout.Wing`, `Fit`, and `FitErrors`, and what is left per generator, the placement of wings and holes (about fifteen lines), differs by the middle wings, the hole list, and the wing names in the fit messages; a shared helper for that remainder would be a parameterised abstraction that risks `005`'s output. *Perimeter Core* groups court trapezoids by orientation bin across all holes; a middle wing wider than `2d` keeps the courts' bands apart, so each bin gives one polygon per court, numbered `P-Court-<Bin>-1`, `-2`, … in the clipping library's union output order: west to east on the canonical plan (the reference observed the court zones' smallest x at 7.43, 43.43, and 79.43 m for three courts), not a court identifier on a rotated plan. Naming them per court would change `Lod.Core`, which the brief did not ask for; ADR-008 leaves it to a later ADR.
8. **Sweeps.** An uncommitted sweep in the reference (50 rotations from 0° to 353.5° with translations, each through the 4 simplifiers × 4 aggregators with 1, 3, 1, 800 runs per configuration) found no failure for the operating suite (2, 3, and 5 banks, and 3 banks with odd dimensions), the court cluster (2, 3, and 4 courts, and 3 courts with odd dimensions), and, as a regression sweep, the enclosed court and the S8.5 families (radial lobes 3 and 4, branching mall 3 and 4, care hub 2, 3, and 4, foyer halls 2 and 3): 18 configurations, 14 400 combinations. The sweep was not replayed.
9. **Smoke-test values.** The values of Task 5 were observed in one scripted headless Rhino 8.25 session (D-056) on the reference's last commit `373af00`, which reported the informational version `0.8.4+373af00…`; they were not re-observed on the replay. Task 5, Step 1 checks that `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` of the branch equal the reference's, so the record applies; the replay found no difference. The viewport, the toolbar icons, the *Inspect* texts beyond the footprint line, the zone IDs, the *Validate* glazing values, and *Convert2BEM* with Override = False were not observed.
10. **Icons.** The 57 existing PNGs are byte-identical after Task 3 (Pillow 12.3.0); the two new ones equal the reference's bytes. The contact-sheet description is from the replay's sheet.
11. **Stray fence lines of S8.5.** The S8.5 plan gave the smoke-test result and the two decision-log entries as code blocks to append; the S8.5 close-out on `main` (`16ead1f`) appended each block's content together with its closing fence line, which left a line of three backticks at the end of the smoke-test guide (line 570) and after D-102 and D-103 in the decision log (lines 705 and 715), so the text after the first of them renders as code. The S8.5 plan and its replay had no check that would catch it: the replay appended the blocks' contents correctly, but compared only the files of the reference, which holds neither the decision log nor the close-out's result section, and checked no code fences. This plan therefore says, wherever text is appended, that the block's content is appended without its fence lines; removes the three stray lines with scripts that first assert exactly where they are (Task 4, Step 8; Task 6, Step 8); and checks every Markdown file of the stage, this plan included, after the documentation commit and before the close-out commit (Task 4, Step 10; Task 6, Step 10): every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, no file ends inside a block, and no decision-log entry and no appended smoke-test section ends with a fence line. In the replay, the scripts of Task 4, Step 8 and Task 6, Step 8 removed exactly lines 570, 705, and 715; the checks of Task 4, Step 10 and Task 6, Step 10 printed the expected output; an independent check written apart from the plan's script found no problem in the twelve files after the close-out merge, and the same independent check run on `main` at `16ead1f` reports the three stray lines (decision log line 705 a bare opening fence, D-102 and D-103 ending with a fence line; smoke-test guide line 570 a bare opening fence, the file ending inside a block).
12. **Clean working tree.** The `git status` expectations assume a working tree with no untracked files outside `.gitignore` before Task 1.
13. **D-numbers.** The reference's report proposed D-104 for the choices of notes 6 and 7, which Tasks 1 and 2 cite, and D-105 for the completion, which cites D-104 and records the fence repair of note 11.
14. **Roadmap order.** The reference report placed the S8.6 Progress line above the S8.5 one; the roadmap lists its Progress lines oldest first, so Task 6 adds it below the S8.5 line, as the S8.5 close-out did for its own.
15. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged as a possible attribution; it states the rule. The `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version of the icons is flagged, as in S4.1 to S8.5.
